626. “uṭaluṟuppukkaḷil kai, vayiṟu eṉpaṉa toṭar ceyaṟpāṭṭiṉatākat tēkkamillāmal ceyaṟ paṭṭāltāṉ uṭal nalam. atupōla oru nirvākat tiṉ aṉaittuṟuppukkaḷum ceyaṟpaṭṭālē nalam vantamaiyum.”

627. “maṉatil uṟuttak kūṭiya etaiyum uriyavarkaḷiṭam collit tīrvu kāṇpatē etir viḷaivu kaḷait tīrkkum.”

628. “taṉṉai vaḷarttuk koḷḷāmal maṟṟavarkaḷ vāḻvatil aḻukkāṟu koḷvatu vāḻum neṟiyaṉṟu.”

629. “tiṭṭamiṭṭa entac ceyalum niṟai nalaṉ tarum.”

630. “paṭittavarkaḷ, paṭikkātavarkaḷ ivarkaḷiṭaiyē nākarikattil periya vēṟupāṭillai.”

631. “oḻuṅkukaḷai kaṭaippiṭittalē veṟṟikaḷ aṉaittiṟkum aṭippaṭai.”

632. “aviyalil pala poruḷkaḷ kalantu tam muḷ cuvaikaḷaip parimāṟṟam ceytukoṇṭu atē pōtu taṉittaṉmaiyaiyum iḻakkāmal iruppatu pōl camutāya amaippum irukkavēṇṭum.”

633. “nēruji irunta iṭattil maṟṟoru varai vaittup pārkka iyalavillai.”

634. “caṭaṅkukaḷiṉāl viḷaiyum payaṉkaḷ yātoṉṟum illai. āṉāl, uṇarvait tūṇṭa caṭaṅkukaḷ tuṇai ceyyum.”

635. “maṉitarkaḷait tiruttak kaṭṭuppāṭukaḷāl iyalātu. maṭai māṟṟaṅkaḷ tēvai.”

636. “toṭarcciyāṉa ceyaṟpāṭē payaṉuṭaiyatu.”

637. “āṇkaḷiṉ ātikkam talaiyeṭutta piṟaku peṇṇaip poruḷākkiṉar.”

638. “peṇmai aruḷum taṉmaiyuṭaiyatu; aravaṇaikkum taṉmaiyuṭaiyatu; poṟuppēṟkum neṟiyuṭaiyatu.”

639. “karuvai vaḷarttu uyarnalam cērttu piṟappikkum peruntavam peṇpāl amaintatē. atuvē peṇ uyarntavaḷ eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟu.”

640. “maṉitaṉ vāḻkkaiyiṉ karaiyōraṅkaḷaik kaṭantu āḻnilaip pakutikkuc ceṉṟālē muḻumai yaṭaivāṉ.”

641. “teppam kaiyakattatāka illāmal pōvatiliruntu pātukākkak karaiyil niṟpavar kayiṟu kaṭṭip piṭittuk koḷvar. atupōla nām ticai teriyāmal pōyviṭātirukkak kaṭavuḷiṭattil nammai kaṭṭip pōṭṭuk koḷḷavēṇṭum.”

642. “tīya paḻakkaṅkaḷāl keṭuvataṟintum tīya paḻakkaṅkaḷiliruntu viṭutalai peṟa muyalātatu iraṅkattakkatu.”

643. “teppattiṟkum, teppakkuḷattiṉ taṇṇīr maṭṭattiṟkum uḷḷa iṭaiveḷi aḷavai viṭa kampu nīḷamāka iruppiṉ akkampu teppattait taḷḷap payaṉpaṭātu. atupōla nirvāka amaippai viṭa nirvāka iyantiram paruttuviṭiṉ nirvākattai iyakka utavi ceyyātu.”

644. “oḷi niṟaik katiravaṉaik kūṭa, nila uruṇṭaiyiṉ niḻal paṭintu maṟaikkiṟatu. atu pōla, pērāṟṟalkaḷum kūṭa, taṟcārpiṉ kāraṇamāka maṟaintu pōkiṟatu.”

645. “nātam-itayattiṉil iyaṅkum mellōcai, itaṉai curappaṭuttuvatu nātacuram.”

646. “maṇiviḻā iraṇṭāvatu cuṟṟukku āyattamāvatu.”

647. “kaṇṇāl pārkkāta vēlai payaṉ tarātu.”

648. “iṉṟaiya irāma - ilakkuvar vi.ji. paṉṉirtās, cantōṣam cakōtararkaḷ.”

649. “āṉmā-uyirē mutal. inta mutalaip perukki vaḷarkkavē uṭal mutaliya aṉaittum.”

650. “maṉitakulam eṉṟa orumai ātmā. inta ātmāvai aṉaivarum vaḷappaṭuttavēṇṭum.”

651. “iḻivāṉatu eṉṟu karutappaṭupavaikaḷai nāṭakaṅkaḷil kāṭṭakkūṭātu,”

652. “paṭittavarkaḷiṉ tavaṟukaḷālēyē varalāṟu kīḻmaippaṭukiṟatu.”

653. “paṭittavarkaḷ paṭippiṟkēṟṟavāṟu tammuṭaiya vāḻkkaip pōkkukaḷai māṟṟik koḷḷātu pōṉāl paṭippiṉāl payaṉ illai.”

654. “taṉimaṉitaṉai uruvākkuvatu avaṉuṭaiya vāḻkālattiṉ camutāya amaippē.”

655. “muṉṉēṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟa pacittuṭippu illāta camutāyam evvaḷavu vacatikaḷ iruntālum muṉṉēṟātu.”

656. “muṉṉēṟṟattiṉ mutaṟpaṭi ārvat tuṭippē!”

657. “naṭaiyil valakkāl - iṭakkāl otticaintu naṭappatupōla, nirvāka amaippu otticaintu iyaṅkavēṇṭum.”

658. “ceyya muṭiyavillai” eṉṟu tiṭṭa miṭavē payappaṭukiṟavarkaḷ oṉṟumē ceyya māṭṭārkaḷ.”

659. “poruḷātāra muṉṉēṟṟam eṉpatu oru vaḻukkal maram. itil kavaṉamāka ēṟavēṇṭum.”

660. “vēlaiyaic ceyyātavarkaḷ, vēlai paṟipōvatai virumpamāṭṭārkaḷ.”

661. “uṭalukkuc calukai taralām, uyirp pātukāppukku muraṇ illāmal! atupōla, camutāya nalaṉukku virōtamillāmal calukai kāṭṭa vēṇṭiya avaciyamiruntāl calukai kāṭṭalām.”

662. “ēḻmai tiṭīreṉa vantataṉṟu. palavita māṉa kēṭukaḷ mutirnta nilaiyil ēḻmai ākiṟatu.”

663. “palavitamāṉa uṇarvukaḷiṉ tokuppē nalvāḻkkai.”

664. “ciṟiyavarkaḷ muraṭṭuttaṉattukkē kaṭṭuppaṭuvārkaḷ.”

665. “etirmaṟai iyakkaṅkaḷ ākkappaṇikaḷ ceyya iyalātu.”

666. “caṭṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷa oṭṭai vaḻit tappittu ōṭiṉāl ōṭupavaṉaik kuṟai collak kūṭātu. caṭṭattaiyē kuṟai collavēṇṭum.”

667. “eppaṭiyō maṉitarkaḷ avarkaḷaic cuṟṟiyē vaṭṭamiṭukiṟārkaḷ! maṟṟavarkaḷaip paṟṟik.” kavalaippaṭuvatākak kūṟuvatukūṭa taṉṉai vaḷarttuk koḷḷattāṉ!”

668. “vayiṟārac cāppiṭa virumpukiṟavarkaḷ uṭalāra uḻaikka muṉvaravēṇṭum.”

669. “uḻaikkāmal uṇpavarkaḷ, maṟṟavar kurutiyaik kuṭippavarkaḷ.”

670. “kāṟṟu aṭikkum ticaiyil maram cāyntāl maram viḻum. kāṟṟai etirttu niṉṟāl vāḻum. atupōl cūḻnilaikaḷaic cārntē vāḻkiṟavarkaḷ vīḻntuviṭuvārkaḷ. cūḻnilaikaḷai etirttu māṟṟa muṉaipavarkaḷē vāḻvārkaḷ.

671. “kavarcciyil mayakkupavavai naṉmai ceytal aritu.”

672. “kuṟikkōḷ taḻiiya vāḻkkai iruntālē tiṭṭamiṭum maṉappōkku tōṉṟum.”

673. tiṭṭamiṭāta ceyalmuṟaikaḷ muḻup payaṉait tarā.”

674. “celvattiṉ ūṟṟu nilamē!”

675. “piṟar paṇattukku, celavukkut tiṭṭa miṭupavarkaḷ eṇṇikkai mikuti.”

676. “kāṟṟu vīcumpoḻutu talai teṟikkaccuṟṟum maraṅkaḷ palamaṟṟavai. atupōla cōtaṉaikaḷ vantuṟṟa poḻutu alamarukiṟavarkaḷ palamaṟṟavarkaḷ.”

677. kaḻivukaḷumkūṭa, poruḷuṟpatti cātaṉaṅkaḷē!”

678. “taṉ muṉaippuṭaiyavarkaḷ kāriyaṅkaḷai viṭṭuviṭuvar.”

679. “uyir vāḻvil uḷḷa ācaiyē nōykaḷai vaḷarkkiṟatu; pātukākkiṟatu.”

680. “kāriyam oṉṟupōlat tōṉṟum. āṉāl, ataṉ kāraṇaṅkaḷ oṉṟalla; palavēyām.”

681. “aḻukkāṟu nuṇmai vaṭivattil iruntālum āṟṟalaik keṭukkum.”

682. “mūḷaic cōmpal ceyaltiṟaṉaik keṭukkum.”

683. “kālattiṉ viḷaivukaḷaiyellām aḷantu eṇṇi aṉupavippavarkaḷ kālattai aḷantu payaṉpaṭuttat tavaṟiviṭukiṟārkaḷ.”

684. “vaḷarcciyillāta araciyal kaṭciyaic cārntavarkaḷ iṉṟu oḻukkak kēṭṭai-muṟaikēṭāṉa atikārap pirayōkattai tēcīya mayappaṭutti viṭṭārkaḷ.”

685. “iṉṟaiya araciyal kaṭcikaḷ-tērtal iyakkaṅkaḷē! araciyal iyakkaṅkaḷ alla!”

686. “iṉṟaiya camutāya amaippil uṭal valimaimē ātikkam ceykiṟatu. aṟivaṟinta āḷumaiyalla.”

687. “oru noṭip poḻutu vāḻkkai niṉṟālum pala nāḷkaḷiṉ muṉṉēṟṟam taṭaippaṭum.”

688. “viraivu mikka iyantira katiyil ulakam pōyk koṇṭirukkiṟatu. āṉāl, intiyarkaḷ āmai vēkattai māṟṟik koṇṭārkaḷillai.”

689. “aracāṅkam vēlaikaḷai uṟpatti ceyyum iyantiramalla. maṉitarkaḷētāṉ vēlaikaḷai uruvākkik koḷḷavēṇṭum.”

690. “ācaikāṭṭi vāṅkuvaṉa ellām oru vakaiyāṉa curaṇṭalēyām.”

691. “veḷḷāṭu vēḷāṇmait tattuvam colvatu pōla, cila pattirikkaikaḷaip paṭitta aḷavilēyē cilar vimarcaṉap pulikaḷākiviṭu kiṉṟaṉar.”

692. “aṇaikkappaṭum pāṅkaip poṟuttē atikāram nilaipeṟum; aṭippatil alla.”

693. “cukattai aṉupavikkum maṉappōkku vanta piṟaku uḻaippāḷiyātal aritu.”

694. “vimarcaṉattiṟku veṭkappaṭupavarkaḷ vaḷarcciyai virumpātavarkaḷ.”

695. “vimarcaṉamum vaḷarcciyum kāraṇa kāriyaṅkaḷ.”

696. “iṉṟaiya aṟivu nalkum karuvikaḷ aṉaittum aṟivu eṉṟa peyaril aṟiyāmaiyaiyē vaḷarkkiṉṟaṉa.”

697. “nilattiṉai muḻutum payaṉpaṭuttum āṟṟalē iṉṉum maṉitaṉukku varavillai.”

698. “iyaṟkaiyai vellum muyaṟciyil maṉitaṉ īṭupaṭavillai. iyaṟkaiyai oṭṭittāṉ vaḷarntu varukiṉṟāṉ.”

699. “iṉṟu cātāraṇamāṉavai eṉṟu karutap peṟupavai aṉaittum uyarntavaikaḷēyām.”

700. “vitikaḷ - accamiṉṟi naṭakka utavuvaṉa. vitikaḷ - accamiṉṟik kāriyaṅkaḷ ceyya utavuvaṉa.”





701. “irāmaṉ taṉakkāka naṭantāṉ. kaṇṇaṉ maṟṟavarkaḷukkāka naṭantāṉ.”

702. “eṉakkut teriyum” eṉṟa muṭivē aṟiyāmaiyiṉ mukaṭu.”

703. “oruvariṭam illāta oṉṟai, avaciyat tēvai karutip peṟṟāl kaiyūṭṭu; kaimāṟṟu; tēvaiyillāta oṉṟaip peṟṟāl atu aṉpiṉ naṭaimuṟai.”

704. “parāmarikkap peṟāta uṟavukaḷ paḻutuṟum.”

705. “pāñcālikku - kaṭaiciyil aḻaitta piṟaku, kaṇṇaṉ utavi ceytataṟkuk kāraṇam pāñcāliyiṉ taṉviṉai muyaṟci mutircciyaṭaiyaṭṭum eṉpataṉālēyām.”

706. “taṉviṉai muyaṟci mutirvaṭaiyātavarkaḷiṭam piṟaviṉai muyaṟcikaḷ tākkaṅkaḷai ēṟpaṭuttip payaṉtarātu.”

707. “taṅkaḷ maṉaivi, makkaḷ, cuṟṟam eṉṟu cuṟṟupavarkaḷ uyartarak kuṇamuṭaiyavarāka iruttal iyalātu.”

708. “eṉṉa-etaṟkākac colliyiruppār eṉṟu āyvu ceyyāmalē oruvar mītu āttirap paṭupavarkaḷ akampāvikaḷ. utavi ceyvataik kaṭamaiyāka vaikkāmal kaṭṭuppāṭāka ākkuvārkaḷ.”

709. “payaṉuṭaiyatāka oruvar ceyyum vēlai amaiyātupōṉāl, atu vipacārattiṟkuc camam.”

ta-6

710. “oḻuṅkuppaṭuttik koḷḷa maṟuppavarkaḷ oṉṟu tavaṟu ceypavarkaḷ. allatu cōmpēṟikaḷ.”

711. “enta oṉṟum tiruppit tarap peṟātu pōṉāl ulakil vaṟaṭci tōṉṟum.”

712. “paṇam varuvatum pōvatum pōtātu. ovvoru kācum varumpoḻutu naṟpaṇpukaḷuṭaṉ atāvatu aṟivaṟinta āḷviṉai-aṉpu mutaliyavaṟṟuṭaṉ vantaṭaiya vēṇṭum.”

713. “tamiḻiṉa orumaippāṭu kāṇpatē cilampiṉ nōkkam.”

714. “perumai vantuṟumpoḻutu cilar aṭaṅkāmal akantai koḷvar. itu uṟavai, orumaippāṭṭaik keṭukkum.”

715. “vaḷḷuvam, cilampu mutaliyaṉa kūṟum uyarnta nōkkaṅkaḷaic camutāyam muṟiyaṭittuviṭṭatu.”

716. “caṭṭaṅkaḷukkut tiruttaṅkaḷ-camutāyat .ētavaikalaḷāka amaiyavēṇṭum. itaik kaṇkāṇittu araciṉ kavaṉattiṟkuk koṇṭuvara caṭṭa ālōcaṉai āṇaikkuḻu tēvai.

717. “vāḻkkaiyiṉ tuṇai nalamāka amaiyum peṇ, aṉupavap poruḷākavum payaṉākavum irukkiṟāḷ.”

718. ñāṉam illātapeṇkaḷ uḷḷa camutāyam cīrkeṭum.”

719. “makkaḷait takutiyuṭaiyavarkaḷāka vaḷarkkum varai nampikkaiyum kaṇkāṇippum iṇaintē irukkavēṇṭum.”

720. camutāya māṟṟaṅkaḷai, cīrtiruttaṅkaḷ mūlamē ceytuviṭa muṭiyātu. cīrtiruttam eṉpatu puṇ nāṟāmalirukkap puṇuku pūcuvatu pōlattāṉ; puṇ āṟātu.”

721. “intiya camūkam, nōy niṟainta camūkam. enta maruntālum nōy tīravē illai. puraṭciyē tēvai.”

722. “āti tirāviṭarkaḷukku eṉṟu taṉit tokuti amaippu tavirkkappaṭṭatu. intiyāviṟku naṉmaiyāyiṟṟu.

723. “kātal ceyyum vāyppu iruntālē camūkattil ellāt tīmaikaḷum pōyviṭum.”

724. vakuppu vāri utavit tiṭṭaṅkaḷ uḷḷa varai cātic caṅkaṅkaḷ vaḷarum.

725. “uttaravātam vaḻaṅkāta aracu uḷḷavarai enta oru oḻukkamum makkaḷ maṉṟattil kāl koḷḷātu.”

726. paṭippataṉāl maṭṭumē naṟpaṇpukaḷ vantu viṭuvatillai. camūka nikaḻvukaḷ tām paṇpāṭṭukkuk kaḷaṉ.”

727. “vakuppu vāri utavit tiṭṭaṅkaḷ uḷḷa varai cātic caṅkaṅkaḷ vaḷarum.”

728. “camūkat takutikaḷē iṉṟuḷḷa piracciṉai. tīṇṭāmaiyalla.”

729. “intiya camūkattiṉ aṭippaṭaik kaṭṭumāṉattilēyē tīṇṭāmai, cāti vēṟṟumai mutaliya tīmaikaḷ paṭintuḷḷaṉa. intat tīmaikaḷai puraṭciyiṉ mūlamē māṟṟa iyalum.”

730. “naṭantu pōṉataṟkuk kavalaip paṭṭup payaṉillai. iṉi naṭakka vēṇṭiyataṟkē kavalaip paṭavēṇṭum”.

731. “piṟappiṉ cārpāl varum kuṇakkēṭukaḷai nīkkavē kalvi-kēḷvi”

732. “ētāvatu or uyar nōkkam vāḻkkaiyai ākkiramikkātavarai kuṟṟaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟu viṭutalai peṟa iyalātu.”

733. “vēlai naṭakkum. āṉāl, naṭanta vēlaikaḷaik koṇṭu paṇi ceytār eṉṟu muṭivu ceyyakkūṭātu. avar vāḻum kālattiṉ tēvai āṟṟal-vāytta karuvikaḷ ākiya aṉaittaiyum kaṇakkil eṭuttuk koṇṭutāṉ aḷavai ceyya vēṇṭum.”

734. “ēḻaikaḷāka vāḻum makkaḷai aṟivuṇarac ceyvatu kaṭumaiyāṉa paṇi.”

735. “iyaṟkaiyiṉ niyati vaḻi, koṇṭum koṭuttum vāḻum muṟaiyē amaintuḷḷatu. maraṅkaḷ maṇṇiliruntu taṇṇir, uram eṭuttuk koṇṭu vaḷarkiṉṟaṉa. piṉ avai paḻutta ilai kaḷai maṇṇukku uramākat tantuviṭukiṉṟaṉa”

736. koṭukkāmal eṭukkum vāḻkkai curaṇṭal vāḻkkai”

737. “vaṟumaiyai aṉupavittup paḻakippōṉa vāḻkkai”

738. “vāḻtal vēṟu vāḻkkaiyiṉ nōkkam vēṟu. vāḻtalukkuriya muyaṟcikaḷ nōkkaṅkaḷaic cārntavaiyākā”

739. “oḻuṅku muṟaiyillāta ceyaṟpāṭukaḷ payaṉait tarā.”

740. “aṟivārnta vāḻkkaiyil piṉtaṅkiya nilai, paṇpāṭṭuk kuṟaivaik kāṭṭum.”

741. “ācaikaḷ niṟaivēṟāmai, vāḻkkaiyiṉ toṭakkaṅkaḷāka amaiyum.”

742. “celavaḻikkap peṟāta kācukaḷum varavēyām.”

743. “payamum camūkat tīmaiyē.”

744. “potumaiyil uruvākum naṉmaikku īṭu illai.

745. “perumpāṉmai uṇarviṉ vaḻippaṭṭa ātikkanilai uṇarvunilai ellāc camūkattiṉarukkum uṇṭu.”

746. “entap paṇikkum mutaṟpaṇi makkaḷaip paṭippikkum paṇiyēyām!”

747. “paḻakiyavarkaḷ ellām naṇparkaḷ allar; paḻakātavarkaḷ ellām pakaivarkaḷum allar.”

748. “uṭal nalattiṟkuc cuṟṟuppuṟac cūḻnilai tēvai. atupōlavē uyir nalattiṟkum cuṟṟuppuṟac cūḻnilai tēvai.”

749. “oruvarukkoruvar māṟik kavalaip paṭṭālē putiya camutāyam tōṉṟiviṭum.”

750. “vāḻat teriyātavarkaḷukkuc ceyyappaṭum utavi pāḻukkiṟaittatākum.”

751. “oruvariṉ iruppu uṇarappaṭum nilaiyē vāḻum nilai.”

752. “uḻaittup peṟāta celvam arumaiyākak karutap peṟamāṭṭātu.”

753. cākkaṭai mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ tēkkattaik kāttirukkum kocu pōla, camūka nikaḻvukaḷil ēṟpaṭum tīmaikaḷaik kāttiruppar kayavar.”

754. “kūṭṭukkaṭai vaittiruppavar tiṭīreṉṟu kūṭṭukkārarukkut teriyāmal taṉikkaṭai vaippatu aiyattiṟku iṭamaḷikkum.

755. “nāttikattiṟku vaḷḷuvam āṇai niṟkātu.”

756. tām ceyyum paṇikaḷai matippīṭu ceyya maṟuppatu, paṇikaḷaip payaṉuṟac ceyya virumpāmaiyē yākum.”

757. “paṇikaḷ pakirvē nirvākam; pakirvukaḷ poṟuppuṭaṉ niṟaivēṟṟappaṭātu pōṉāl noymmaiyē tōṉṟum.”

758. “nāṭṭu makkaḷait toḻilāḷarkaḷākavē ākkavēṇṭum; kaṭaṉkārarkaḷākavum viyāpārikaḷākavum ākkakkūṭātu.”

759. “uṟpattiyoṭu toṭarpillāta etuvum puṇṇiyamāṉa kaṭamaiyākātu.”

760. “nalla paṇi, viḷamparam illā malart tōṭṭam.”

761. “oḻuṅkuppaṭuttap paṭāta ceyalāl oṉpatu naṭakka vēṇṭiya iṭattil oṉṟutāṉ naṭakkum.”

762. “nitāṉamaṟṟavarkaḷ, maṟṟavarkaḷiṉ māṉattai, kīrttiyaip paḻikkiṉṟaṉar.”

763. “enta oṉṟum tavirkka iyalātatu eṉṟu ākiviṭṭāl anta aḷavil aṭimaittaṉam vantu viṭukiṟatu.”

764. “nallavarkaḷaiyē meḷaṉam kavarccikkum, maṟṟavarkaḷai ārpparavamē kavarccikkum.”

765. “nākarikam cīrkulainta camutāyattil poymmaiyē vāymai.”

766. “ulakiyal peyarāl anītikkum uṭaṉpaṭa vēṇṭi varukiṟatu.”

767. “pēy aracu ceytāl pēraṟamum kuṉṟu kiṟatu.”

768. “vāḻkkaiyiṉ tēvaikaḷ kuṟikkōḷ ākā. vāḻkkaiyiṉ tēvaikaḷaiyum kaṭantatu kuṟikkōḷ.”

769. “maṉitaṉ paḻakka valaiyiṉiṉṟu mīḷa maṟukkiṟāṉ.”

770. “piriviṉaikaḷum oru vakaiyil tīṇṭāmaiyē.”

771. “paṇam koṭuppataiviṭa, paṇattai campātippavaṉāka ākkutal uyariya paṇi.”

772. “matam makkaḷiṉ aṉṟāṭa vāḻkkaiyil aṉuṭṭāṉattil iruntāl eppōtum aḻiyātu.”

773. “nallavarkaḷukku irukkum āṟṟal, paṭaikkum āṟṟal. keṭṭavarkaḷukku irukkum āṟṟal, aḻikkum āṟṟal.”

774. “naṉmaiyaic ceytāl maṭṭum pōtātu. yārukkākac ceyyappaṭukiṟatō avarkaḷ naṉmaiyai aṟintukoḷḷa vēṇṭum."

775. “ellārum pukaḻai-perumaiyai virumpukiṟārkaḷ. atait tārāḷamāka maṟṟavarkaḷukku vaḻaṅkuka.”

776. “nallavaṇṇam makkaḷ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkup paṇi ceyyum niṟuvaṉattiṟku ātaravu iruppa tillai.”

777. “arimā caṅkam, cuḻaṟkuḻuk kaḻakam pōṉṟa potu amaippil iruppavarkaḷ, piriviṉaikaḷai vaḷarttu uṟavaik keṭukkum cātic caṅkaṅkaḷ, mata amaippukaḷil paṅku peṟutal kūṭātu.”

778. “iṉṟu ellā vakaiyāṉa paṇikaḷilum ciṟantatu, makkaḷiṭattil vāḻvu viruppaṅkaḷaiyum urimaikaḷaiyum uṇarttutal.”

779. “viḷamparattālō, calukaikaḷālō pēccālō vākkukaḷ vāṅkappaṭum varai makkaḷāṭci muṟai ciṟakkātu.”

780. “paṇattiṉ arumai aṟital vēṟu. paṇattiṉ mītu ācai vaittal vēṟu.”

781. “paṇattiṉ mītu viruppam koṇṭu, īṭṭi, muṟaiyākap payaṉpaṭuttutalē nalvāḻkkai.”

782. “ulaka orumai, pūta peḷatīkaṅkaḷāl ēṟpaṭṭirukkiṟatē tavira, āṉmīka rītiyāka ēṟpaṭa villai.”

783. “aṟiviyal ulakattai iṇaittatu. uyirkkulattai iṇaikkavillai.”

784. “matam iyakkamāka-vāḻkkai muṟaiyāka irunta varaiyil makkaḷiṉ vāḻkkai nilaiyai uyarttap payaṉpaṭṭatu. matattiṟkut talaivarkaḷum purōkitarkaḷum uruvāṉārkaḷē aṉṟiliruntu matam, makkaḷukku naṉmai ceyyavillai. tīmaiyē ceytatu: ceytuvarukiṟatu.”

785. “maṟantum ciṟṟellaikaḷil maṉitaṉ viruppam koḷḷakkūṭātu; viruppam kāṭṭakkūṭātu. itu vaḷarcciyai-āṉmāviṉ viṭutalaiyai taṭukkum.”

786. “pollāta maṉitaṉ uruvākkiya ellaikaḷiṉṟu viṭutalai peṟutalē uṇmaiyāṉa viṭutalai.”

787. “namatu iru cevikaḷ oṉṟaiyoṉṟu nōkkātu etirtticaiyil amaintiruppatiṉ nōkkamē iru pakkaṅkaḷaiyum kēḷeṉpataṟkākavē.”

788. “aṟivārnta aṉpē, vāḻkkaikkup payaṉpaṭum.”

789. “uṟpattik karuvikaḷiṉ vaḷarcciyāl maṉita vāḻnilai uyarntatu.”

790. “pōrk karuvikaḷiṉ vaḷarcciyāl maṉita nākarikam pāḻpaṭṭatu. maṉitakulam aḻiviṉ ellaikkuc ceṉṟu koṇṭirukkiṟatu.”

791. “nāṭu, moḻi, iṉam, matam ākiyaṉavaṟṟaik kaṭantatu orumaippāṭu.”

792. “orumaippāṭu amaiyāta nāṭṭil vaḷamāṉa vāḻkkai amaiyātu.”

793. “caṭṭattiṉ cila vitikaḷai amulpaṭuttum atikārattai atikāravarkkattiṟku vaḻaṅkakkūṭātu."

794. “cila caṭṭa vitikaḷai nīkkutal iyalātu. āṉāl, payaṉpaṭuttutal tavirkkappaṭutal vēṇṭum.”

795. “iṉṟaiya araciyalai, taṉṉala nayappum uṇarcciyumē iyakkukiṉṟaṉa.”

796. “intiyāvil tavaṟukaḷaip poṟuttalla, etirppu, iyakkaṅkaḷ. pātikkappaṭṭavarkaḷiṉ kūccal pōṭum tiṟamaiyaip poṟuttatu.”

797. “intiya mutalāḷikaḷ piriviṉai caktikaḷai vaḷarkkiṉṟaṉar, ēḻmaiyiṉ tuṉpattāl tuyaruṟuvōṉ puraṭciyil īṭupaṭāmal irukka”

798. “intiyāvil kaṭcikaḷ vaḷarntuḷḷaṉa. araciyal vaḷara villai.”

799. “maṉitaṉai, maṉitaṉāka matippatu oḻukkam.”

800. “orumaippāṭṭuṇarvu eṉpatu tavattiṉ tavam.”





801. “ciṉṉa ciṉṉa ceytikaḷil kāṭṭap peṟum akkaṟaiyē periya periya cātaṉaikaḷaic ceyya utavi ceyyum.”

802. “atikāram uṭaiyavarkaḷ naṉkoṭai kēṭṭāl kūṭa atu nirppantāṉ.”

803. “tīmaikaḷ koluviruntu pārāṭṭukkaḷ peṟum yukattil nallaṉa nikaḻtal arumai.”

804. “maruntākap payaṉpaṭum kōpam, tītaṉṟu. varavēṟkattakkatu.”

805. “ovvoruvarum tam āṟṟal muḻuvataiyum uḻaippāka māṟṟiṉāl vaḷam perukum."

806. “kūṭṭuṟavu, poruḷātāra vaḷarcci iyakkam maṭṭum alla. aḵtu or āṉmīka vaḷarcci iyakkamum ākum.

807. “eṅku, poṟuppukaḷ pakirntaḷikkap peṟavillaiyō, aṅku uḻaippu muḻumaiyākātu, kālakkēṭum uḻaippuk kēṭum irukkum.”

808. “uḻaippu, uḻaippukkākavē-vēlai, vēlaikkākavē eṉṟu ceytalē oḻukkam.”

809. “oḻukkattiṉ tāy nalla uḻaippu.”

810. “aḻukkāṟu, āṇavap pōṭṭikaḷ ākkattaik keṭukkum.”

811. “nilam uḻaippukkēṟṟa payaṉai oḷikkāmal vaḻaṅkukiṟatu.”

812. “payaṉpaṭuttāta nilai pāḻ. payaṉpaṭāta maṉitaṉum pāḻ.”

813. “arppaṇippu nilaiyil vāḻum tāvara iṉaṅkaḷ pōṟṟutalukkuriyaṉa.”

814. “vēḷāṇmaiyil namatu muṉṉōr piṉ paṟṟiya muṟaikaḷ tīmai payavātu, nalam ceytaṉa: atāvatu iyaṟkai uraṅkaḷ etir viḷaivukaḷai uṇṭākkātu.”

815. “paṇam-ūkkattilum paṇpāṭṭukkuriya aṟa ūkkam uyarntatu.”

816. “uṟpatti nukarvukkē ilāpattiṟku alla.”

817. “nōyiṉṟi vāḻa, kāykaṉikaḷē ciṟanta uṇavu.”

818. “maṇṇil kuṟai illai! enta maṇṇaiyum maṉitaṉ payaṉpāṭṭukkuk koṇṭu varalām.”

819. “maṇṇil viḷaiyum poruḷkaḷil maṉitaṉukkup payaṉpaṭāta kaḻivukaḷaiyē maṇ virumpukiṟatu. kaḻivup poruḷkaḷaiyum ākkamāka māṟṟum āṟṟal maṇṇukku irukkiṟatu.”

820. “maṉitaril kaṭaiyāya maṉitarai mēmpāṭuṟac ceytalilēyē ñāṉiyiṉ arumai veḷippaṭum.”

821. “nilam camūkattiliruntu kuṟaivākap peṟṟuk koḷkiṟatu. niṟaiya tiruppik koṭukkiṟatu. ituvē niyati. maṉitaṉ camūkattiliruntu niṟaiya eṭuttuk koḷḷavē virumpukiṟāṉ.”

822. “pēṇappaṭupavaḷ peṇ; maṟṟavarkaḷaip pēṇip pātukāppavaḷ peṇ.”

823. maṉitaṉ eḷitil maṟakka maṟukkiṟāṉ, āṉāl pala naṉmaikaḷai maṟantu viṭukiṟāṉ. naṉṟallātatai maṟakka maṟukkiṟāṉ.”

824. “peṇkaḷukkē, peṇṇaip perumaip paṭuttat teriyavillai.”

825. “peṇkaḷ, tam iṉappakai uṭaiyavarkaḷ.”

826. “māppiḷḷaikku (āṇukku) vāykkum māmiyār nallavaḷ! āṉāl peṇṇukku appaṭip paṭṭa māmiyār vāyppatu aritu.”

827. “maṉitaṉ eḷitil uṇarccivacappaṭak kūṭātu. kāraṇa, kāriyaṅkaḷai ārāyntu pārkka vēṇṭum.”

828. “maṉitaṉ taṉṉuṭaiya periya tavaṟukaḷaik kūṭa niyāyappaṭuttukiṟāṉ. maṟṟavarkaḷuṭaiya ciṟiya piḻaiyaikkūṭa peritupaṭuttukiṟāṉ.”

829. “jaṉanāyakam veṟṟi peṟa araciyal vātikkuk koḷkai, kōṭpāṭu uṟuti ākiyaṉa tēvai.”

830. “peṇ perumaikkuriya piṟappu.”

831. “etirpārttuc ceytal eṉpatē ciṟanta ceyaṟpāṭu.”

832. “vaitīkap pūcaṉaiyai viṭa, āyiram maṭaṅku payaṉuṭaiyatu aṉpuvaḻipāṭu.”

833. “ceytit tāḷkaḷ tarum ceytikḷaik koṇṭu naṉmai, tīmaikaḷai muṭivu ceyya iyalātu; kūṭātu.”

834. “kuṭiyāṭciyiṉ ciṟappu, kuṭimakkaḷukkum aracāṅkattiṟkum uḷḷa nerukkamāṉa uṟavukaḷaip poṟuttatēyām.”

835. “aracāṅkattiṉ nitiyaip pōṟṟat teriyaviṭṭāl valimaiyāṉa aracāṅkam amaiyātu.”

836. “naṉmaikaḷē kūṭa piṟitoru kālattil tīmaiyāka māṟi viṭuvatuṇṭu. viḻippu tēvai.”

837. “kūṭṭuṟavu iyakkaṅkaḷ, pañcāyattukkaḷ nāṭṭai uyirppu nilaiyil vaittuk koḷḷa utavi ceyvaṉa.”

838. “vivacāyikaḷukkuk kaṭaṉ vajā! maṟupuṟam kaḷḷukkaṭai vacūl!”

851. “eḷiyar, uyarvu pārāṭṭutalum, mika uyarvuṭaiyōr tammai eḷiyarākkik koḷḷutalum ulakattil kāṇappeṟum kāṭci.”

852. “uṟavai vaḷarttup pēṇutal peruvāḻ viṉait tarum.”

853. “pukaḻil mayaṅkāta māṉiṭar illai. pukaḻvatil tārāḷamāka naṭantu koḷvatu uyarvataṟku vaḻi”

854. “uṭalōṭu muraṇpaṭṭu viḷaiyāṭiṉāl nōyiṉāl taṇṭittu viṭukiṟatu.”

855. “iḻivāka eṇṇi alaṭciyam ceytāl, etirviḷaiyāka aṟivait tarukiṟatu, uṭal.”

856. “ovvoru ceyalukkum ataṟkuriya payaṉaik kaṇakkiṭal uḻaippiṉai ūkkuvikkum”

857. “intiya vāḻkkaiyamaippil ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷa cātip piriviṉaikaḷ eḷitil nīṅkātu. piriviṉai vaḻippaṭṭa pakaiyait taṇittālē pōtum.”

858. “iṉṟu paraspara nampikkaiyaṟṟavarkaḷē kūṭṭāḷikaḷ.”

859. “avaravar viṉaivaḻi avaravar” eṉṟa viti immiyum piṟaḻāmal nikaḻvatai es.ṭi.es-em.ji.ār. mōtalil uyttuṇara muṭikiṟatu.

860. “takuti uṭaiyavarkaḷaiviṭat takutiyillātavarkaḷē uyara virumpukiṉṟaṉar.”

861. “ovvoru paḻakkamāka naṟpaḻakkaṅkaḷ vāḻkkaiyil uṟutippaṭṭālkūṭa eḷitil veṟṟi peṟalām.”

862. “potu makkaḷ ēmāḷikaḷāka irukkum varai āṭciyāḷarkaḷ yōkkiyarkaḷāka māṭṭārkaḷ.”

863. “paṇattiṉ matippu, allatu vilai orē mātiriyāka irukkum eṉṟu karutit tiṭṭamiṭutal kūṭātu. paṇa vīkkam kavaṉattil irukkavēṇṭum.

864. “uṟpattiyāḷaṉāka māṟiṉāl oḻiya vāḻkkait taram nilaittu niṟkātu.”

865. “oruvarai maṟaittu oruvar collum coṟkaḷaik kēḷātoḻika!

866. “taṟcalukaiyaṟṟa cuyamatippīṭṭilum ciṟanta āciriyaṉ illai.”

867. “uṇarvu pūrvamāṉa ceyalmuṟaippaṭṭa camūka vāḻvu tōṉṟiṉāl camutāyam naṉṟāka amaiyum.”

868. “āravāra araciyalil uḷḷa kavarcci ākkap paṇikaḷukku iruppatillai.”

869. “vināṭikaḷaik kaṇakkiṭṭuk kāriyaṅkaḷ ceytalē vāḻum muṟai.”

870. “aṉṟāṭa vāḻkkaiyil tirukkōyilaiyum camūkattaiyum iṇaittu vāḻtalē ciṟanta vāḻvu.”

871. “uyirppum uṇarvum ceyalil kalakkumāṉāl veṟṟi uṟuti.”

872. “tiṭṭamiṭum poḻutē etirkāla varalāṟṟup pōkkai uyttuṇarntu tiṭṭamiṭal nallatu.”

873. “taṉ kāriya nōkkuṭaiya cuyanalamikaḷ, aṭuttavar vīṭṭai iṭittut taṉ vīṭṭaik kaṭṭuvar.”

874. “naṭaimuṟaic cillaṟaic celavukaḷil kavaṉam mūlataṉattaik kāppāṟṟa utavum.”

875. “kaṇṇukkum karuttukkum varātu maṟaintu koṇṭirukkum paṇikaḷait tēṭic ceyvatēpaṇi.”

876. “uṉṉiṭamuḷḷa paṇikaḷil nī ceyya vēṇṭiya nilaiyāṉatait tērnteṭuttu uṭaṉ cey.”

877. “piṟar colvataik kēṭṭuc cintikkātavarkaḷ mūrkkarkaḷākavē iruppar.”

878. “namakku vāyppuk koṭukkāmalē nammītu paḻi cumattupavarkaḷ uṇṭu. viḻippāka iru.”

879. “namatu ceyaṟpāṭṭil - maṟṟavarkaḷ uṇarvu pūrvamāka īṭupāṭu koḷḷac ceyvatilēyē veṟṟi irukkiṟatu.”

880. “nallavarkaḷ vallavarkaḷāka iruntāl tāṉ, kāriyam nikaḻum.”

881. “cātāraṇamāka makkaḷ eḷitil uṇmai nilaikku varuvatillai.”

882. “cuvai, uṇṇum kiḷarcciyait tarukiṟatu. āṉāl, naṉmai payappatillai.”

883. “pulāl uṇavait tavirkkak kūṭiya vaḻipirāṉṉikaḷai viruppuṭaṉ vaḷarttalēyām.”

884. “ūkkattuṭaṉ ceyyap peṟāta vēlai, arai vēlai.”

ta-7

885. “ēḻaikaḷ, eḷitil etaiyum viṟpar.”

886. “iṉṟaiya āciriyarkaḷukkup poṟuppu uṇarvu vantuviṭṭāl nāṭṭiṉ muṉṉēṟṟam uṟuti.”

887. “jaṉanāyaka amaippu eṉpatu kalantu pēcuvatu eṉpatu. āṉāl, anta amaippiṟkkuḷḷēyē oruvarai oruvar purintu koḷḷāmalēyē palar uḷḷaṉar.”

888. “iṉiyaṉa pēcutal ellāvaṟṟaiyum kūṭṭuvikkum.”

889. “kālattai oḻuṅkuppaṭutti - kālattiṟkuriya paṇikaḷaiyum oḻuṅkuppaṭuttiṉāl niṟaiyap paṇikaḷaic ceyyalām.”

890. “paṭaippāṟṟal uṭaiya kalviyē kalvi.”

891. “kaṟṟuk koṇṭē iruppatu aṟi āṟṟiṉait tuārkkāmal tantu koṇṭirukkum.”

892. “toḻiṟ puraṭci ēṟpaṭum nāṭē vaḷarum - vāḻum.”

893. “pukaḻntē kāriyam cātittuk koḷḷum makkaḷ putticālikaḷ.”

894. “taṟkālikat tēvaikaḷai ottip pōṭṭāl nīṇṭa kālat tēvaikaḷai aṭaiyalām.”

895. “celvattai mutalīṭākkum muyaṟciyē muyaṟci.”

896. “camūkattil tāykku irukkum poṟuppu uṇarvu, tantaikku varuvatillai."

897. “acaṭṭut tuṇiccal - avalattaiyē tarum. aṟivārāynta tuṇivu veṟṟikaḷaik kuvikkum.”

898. “piṟarukkuk koṭukkātē eṉakkuk koṭu eṉpavaṉ, tēvaikkuk kēṭkavillai, aṭuttavaṉukkuk kiṭaikkak kūṭātu eṉpataṟkākavē kēṭkiṟāṉ.”

899. “viruppattuṭaṉ uḻaittalē uyar vāḻvukku aṭittaḷam.”

900. “camutāya māṟṟaṅkaḷ nikaḻttum āṟṟalē payaṉuṭaiya āṟṟal.”





901. “iṉṟaiya tērtal muṟaiyil-puttarāl kūṭa lañcam vāṅkāmal irukkamuṭiyātu.”

902. “enta oru tīmaiyum - marunteṉat toṭaṅkip paḻakkamākiviṭṭaṉavē yām.”

903. “veḷippaṭaiyāka namatu uṟavaic collik koḷḷa veṭkappaṭupavarkaḷ cuya lāpattukkāka maṭṭumē uṟavu kāṭṭukiṟārkaḷ.”

904. “āciriyar, māṇākkarai aṟivāl vaḷarkkum kaṭamai uṭaiyavar.”

905. “cūḻnilaikaḷ mītu paḻi pōṭukiṟavarkaḷ ceyalaṟṟavarkaḷ.”

906. “cūḻnilaikaḷai etirttup pōrāṭuvatē vāḻkkaiyiṉ vaḷarccikku utti.”

907. “palaṉ etirpārkkāmal ceyyum paṇiyē paṇiyākum.”

908. “mūṉṟukāl oṭṭam eṉṟu oṉṟu uṇṭu. uṭaṉpāṭillātavarai aṉaittuk koṇṭu vāḻkkaiyai naṭattuvatu pōṉṟatu.”

909. “vāḻkkaiyil taṭaikaḷ ēṟpaṭuvatu vaḷarkkum aruḷippāṭēyām.”

910. “paṭittavarkaḷē camūkanikaḻviṟkup poṟup pēṟkiṟārkaḷ. iṉṟaiya camūka nikaḻvu naṉṟāka illai. ātalāl, āciriyarkaḷ tam paṇiyaic ciṟappākac ceyyavillai.”

911. “āṅkilam ulakap potumoḻi eṉpatu uṇmaiyalla.”

912. “kaṭavuḷ nampikkai, vāḻkkaikkut tuṇai ceyyāta poḻutu nāstikam tōṉṟukiṟatu.”

913. “nāstikam kaṭavuḷ illai eṉpatillai. āriya mata nūlkaḷai ārāyntavarkaḷukkuk kiṭaitta paṭṭam.”

914. “māṇavarkaḷiṭattil tāyiṉum iṉiya pācam illātavarkaḷ āciriyap paṇiyil veṟṟi peṟa iyalātu.”

915. “nalla nūlkaḷai kaṟpatu avaciyam.”

916. “kītai, varṇa vēṟṟumaikaḷaik kāppāṟṟum nūl.”

917. “pāratam ātipattiya caṇṭaiyai vivarikkum nūl.”

918. “eriyāta vēḷvit tīyai muyaṉṟu eriya vaittu maṉaniṟaivu koḷvatu pōla, camutāya cīrtiruttattilum muyaṟci tēvai.”

919. “caṭaṅkukaḷāl eḷitil makkaḷ kūṭukiṟārkaḷ.”

920. “kālan tāḻtti varuvatu keḷaravam eṉṟāṉa piṟaku nāṭu uruppaṭātu.”

921. “iṟaiyaruḷāl kūṭṭuvikkum maṉam. ātalāl tirumaṇam.”

922. “māyā vāta, peḷatta, camaṇak kalappiṟkup piṟakē tamiḻar vāḻkkaiyil vāḻkkaiyaip paṟṟiya iḻivu uṇarvu tōṉṟiyatu.”

923. “tantai-tāyār koṇṭāṭuvatu piṟanta nāḷ. maṉaivi koṇṭāṭuvatu tirumaṇa nāḷ. camutāyam koṇṭāṭuvatu payaṉ kaṇṭa nāḷ allatu potuvāka iṟanta nāḷ.”

924. “araciyalvātikaḷ cuṟṟulā varāta kirāmaṅkaḷil vēlai ceyvatu eḷitu.”

925. “aṇṇā kaiyil putiya a.ti.mu.ka. koṭi. ettaṉai koṭikaḷaittāṉ avar piṭippār!”

926. “payiṟciyil vaḷarvatu paṇpāṭu.”

927. “peṇkaḷāl orumaippāṭṭai vaḷarkka muṭiyum.”

928. “āṇukku aṉpuṭaiya māmaṉum māmiyum vāyttu viṭukiṟārkaḷ! peṇṇukku appaṭi vāyppatillai.”

929. “kātal malarvataṟkuriya vāyilkaḷ iṉṟu illai.”

930. “oru nilaippaṭṭu oruvarukkut tuṇaiyāka arppaṇippu uṇarvuṭaṉ vāḻvatu kaṟpunilai.

931. “ceṉṉai nakarattil maṭṭum 119000 ēḻaikaḷ vāḻkiṟārkaḷ”.

932. “aḻivu - kaḻivu ulakattiyaṟkaiyaṉṟu. etaiyum paṭaippāṟṟal uṭaiyatākkalām.”

933. “nammaic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa uṟpatti cātaṉaṅkaḷai muḻumaiyākap payaṉpaṭuttum pāṅku vantu viṭṭālē vaḷam koḻikkum.”

934. “akattiṉ vaṟumaiyē puṟattu vaṟumaiyaip paṭaikkiṉṟatu.”

935. “tērtalil veṟṟi peṟuvataiyē nōkkamāka uṭaiya araciyalvātikaḷ nāṭṭai uyartta māṭṭārkaḷ,”

936. “calukaikaḷ camutāyattai vaḷarkkum caktiyākā.”

937. “tamiḻiṉam, eṉṟum orumaippaṭṭallai.”

938. “uṭaṉpiṟappu, irattattiṉ irattam ākiya coṟkaḷai nikaḻvukaḷ poruḷaṟṟatākki viṭṭaṉa.”

939. “oḻuṅkukaḷ nalam payappaṉavēyaṉṟit tuṉpam taruvaṉa alla.”

940. “viti vilakkukaḷ viṉai viḷaikkalaṉkaḷēyām.”

941. “piṟar varunta vāḻtal aṟamākātu.”

942. “kīḻāṉa maṉitarkaḷaikkūṭa aṅkīkāram aḷippataṉ mūlam tiruttalām.”

943. “paṭittavarkaḷiṉ paṇpāṭṭāl pār muḻuvatum tiruntiviṭum.”

944. “maṉitaṉukku iyaṟkai vaḻaṅkiya mutal mūlapporuḷ kālamēyām. mutal uṟpattik karuvi uṭalēyām.”

945. “iṉṟu tīya paḻakkaṅkaḷ mūccuk kāṟṟāki viṭṭaṉa.”

946. “ceyalkaḷ payaṉtarā. ceyalkaḷukkuriya nōkkaṅkaḷē payaṉtarum.”

947. “uṇarccikaḷ nīriṟkumiḻi; uṇarvukaḷ alaikaḷ; ārvam ūṟṟu.”

948. “maṉitaṉiṉ mutal naṇpaṉ cuṟucuṟuppē.”

949. “oṉṟumē ceyyātavaṉai viṭa, payaṉaṟṟa vaikaḷaic ceypavaṉ paravāyillai.”

950. “camūkattiṭam pōkātē; pōṉapiṟaku tayaṅkātē”

951. “uṉṉiṭam maṟṟavarkaḷaip paṟṟi kuṟai kūṟukiṟavarkaḷiṭam viḻippāyiru. iṭam koṭukkātē.”

952. “celvattaic celavaḻitta vakaiyum, aḷavum, uṭaṉukkuṭaṉ pārppatu vīṇ celavait tavirkkavum, celvattaic cērkkavum, tuṇai ceyyum.”

953. “poruḷuṟpattik kuṇam vaḷarntālē vaiyakam vaḷarum; vāḻum.”

954. “uṭal tēvaiyai īṭu ceyyum vāyum vayiṟum ulakameṅkum orē nilaiyiṉavāka amaintiruppatē cōcalica camutāya amaippiṉ niyāyattiṉai valiyuṟuttukiṟatu.”

955. “uḻaippil pātiyē nukartaṟku uriyatu eṉpatāl iraṇṭu kai; oru vayiṟu.”

956. “poṟuppai uṇarttukiṟavarkaḷ potiyaic cumappārkaḷ.”

957. “taṉimaṉitarkaḷukkiṭaiyil uruvākum pakaimaiyil kūṭa oruvar oruvarai aḻikka vāyppillāmal taṭuppatē aracu.”

958. “irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭil nerukkaṭikaḷai araciṉ tuṇai koṇṭu māyāvātam tamiḻ neṟiyai muṟiyaṭikkiṟatu.”

959. “avaravar nalaṉil uḷḷa akkaṟai aḷavukku, avaravar vaḷarccikku aṭippaṭaiyāka amaiyum potu nalaṉil akkarai ēṟpaṭṭāl ulaku ceḻikkum.”

960. “marattiliruntu koytu eṭukkakkūṭātu, kaṉi. koyyakkaṉi'yāyiṟṟu.”

961. “ovvoruvarum taṉittaṉiyē avarkaḷ vaḻaṅkum aṉpai aṟiyavēṇṭum eṉṟu ārvam kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉar. kālam, ottuḻaippatillai.”

962. “etirppu nallatu; etiri nallataṉṟu.”

963. “tīmai ceyya valimai avaciyamillai. ciṉṉac ciṉṉap pūccikaḷ kūṭa nōyai perukku kiṉṟaṉa.”

964. “uṭal varunta uḻaittālē, nōy, aṇukātu.”

965. “āṅkila vaittiya muṟaiyil pattiyam. illai. pattiyamillā vaittiyam nōyait taṇikkalām. nōyiṉ kāraṇaṅkaḷ nīṅkā.”

966. “ēḻaikaḷukkut tuṉpam paḻakip pōṉatāl muṉṉēṟṟattaip paṟṟik kavalaippaṭa maṟukkiṟārkaḷ.”

967. “paṇam campātippavarkaḷ tam kāriyattilēyē kuṟiyāka iruppārkaḷ; nām ēmāṟak kūṭātu.”

968. “celvattiṉ arumai teriyātavarkaḷ celaviṉaṅkaḷaip paṟṟi kavalaippaṭa māṭṭārkaḷ.”

969. “coṉṉataic ceyyavē naparkaḷaik kāṉōm. cuya cintaṉaiyuṭaiyavarkaḷ eṅṅaṉam kiṭaippārkaḷ?”

970. “kaṭaṉaik kaḻippatu eṉṟa uṇarvil ceyyap peṟum vēlai payaṉ tarātu.”

971. “maruntumalai, maruttuvariṭam ceṉṟatu pōl civāccāriyārkaḷ mantira māyaṅkaḷukkuc celkiṉṟaṉar.”

972. “kavalai’kku iṭam taruvatu aḻivukku vaḻi.”

973. “karu uṟṟirukkum tāy, piracava vētaṉaikku āḷāṉālē kuḻantai piṟakkum; atupōla muṉṉēṟa virumpukiṟavarkaḷ pōrāṭṭa uṇarvuṭaṉ kaṭumaiyāka uḻaikkavēṇṭum.”

974. “vēlai vāṅkuvataiviṭa vēlai ceyvatu kaṭiṉam.”

975. “peṇ valiyavē aṭimaiyākiṟāḷ.”

976. “vayiṟu illātu pōṉāl palar uḻaikkavē māṭṭārkaḷ.”

977. “maṟṟavar tollai aṟiyātavarkaḷ mūṭarkaḷ.”

978. “kālattai aḷantu celavaḻippōrē paṇattai eṇṇi vāṅkum urimaiyuṭaiyavar āvar.”

979. “kālaṅkaṭanta kaṭamaikaḷ cumaiyāki viṭum.”

980. “iṉṟu paṭṭaṅkaḷukkum paṇpāṭṭukkum uṟavillai.”

981. “tiṭṭamiṭṭap paṇikaḷukkuc celavaḻittalē muṟaiyākum.”

982. “muntirik koṭṭai pōl turuttikkoṇṭu muṉvarupavarkaḷ, kaṇkāṇikkappaṭa vēṇṭiyavarkaḷ.”

983. “kāriya cātaṉaiyē cātaṉai.”

984. “taṉṉuṭalukku, tāṉē maruttuvar.”

985. “ākātatai aṟavē maṟantiṭutal naṉṟu.”

986. “cātāraṇa maṉitaṉkūṭa uṭaluḻaippukku kūccappaṭukiṟāṉ.”

987. “paḷḷik kūṭam, tirukkōyilaip pōl pēṇap peṟutal vēṇṭum.”

988. “āciriyar-māṇākkarkaḷiṉ kūṭṭu muyaṟciyilēyē kalviyiṉ tiṟaṉ amaiyum.”

989. “vāḻkkai muḻutum nūl paṭikkum paḻakkam toṭariṉ, iṉpam payakkum.”

990. “maṉappāṭam maṭṭumē kalvi eṉṟa nilai amaiyiṉ, cintaṉai ūṟṟu, tūrntu pōkum.”

991. “nēriṭaiyākak kalvi pōtippataiviṭa, ceymuṟaikaḷ mūlam paṭippittal nallatu.”

992. “eḻucci niṟainta paṭaippāṟṟal mikkatē kalvi.”

993. “āciriyarkaḷiṉ poṟuppiṉmaiyiṉālēyē, camūka varalāṟṟil taram kuṟaintu varukiṟatu.”

994. “maṉitaṉiṉ mutalum, muṭivumāṉa tēvai aṟivē.”

995. "paṭippaṟivai viṭa, paṭṭaṟivu atikap payaṉ tarum.”

996. “puttakaṅkaḷilum pōtaṉāk karuvikaḷ vilai matippuḷḷavai.”

997. “vakuppaṟaikkuḷ pōtippataiviṭa vakuppaṟaikku veḷiyē niṟaiya kaṟpikkalām.”

998. “uṇmaiyaik kaṇṭupiṭittal eḷitaṉṟu.”

999. “cūḻnilaikku iraiyātal vilaṅku iyal. cūḻnilaiyait taṉakku ēṟṟavāṟu amaittuk koḷḷap pōrāṭutal maṉita iyal.”

1000. “uṭampiṟku uḻaippiṉmai, nōy. uṟavukku, aḻukkāṟu nōy.”





1001. “oṟṟumai nallatutāṉ! āṉāl, etaṟkāka oṟṟumai? aṭutta camūkattiṉ mītu tākkutal ceyya oṟṟumai uruvāṉāl naṉṟaṉṟu. nāṭu, ūr aḷavil oṟṟumai uruvātalil oraḷavu naṉmai uṇṭu.”

1002. “tuṉpaṅkaḷait tavirttuk koḷḷātavaṉ aṟivillātavaṉ.”

1003. “pāratam” toṭarkiṟatu ottivaippatil.”

1004. “kaiyūṭṭukaḷ koṭuttu kāriyam cātikkum vacatiyuḷḷavarkaḷ uḷḷavaraiyil, pūṭṭiṉai oḻikka muṭiyātu.”

1005. “aracu aluvalakaṅkaḷ cuṟucuṟuppāka iyaṅkiṉāl kaiyūṭṭait tavirkkalām.”

1006. “tērtalil paṇam viḷaiyāṭuvatait tavirttālē, nāṭu vāḻum.”

1007. “araciyalvātikaḷ vāṅkum ilañcamē, nāṭṭil ilañcattai vaḷarkkiṟatu.”

1008. “vilaivāci ēṟṟam araciṉ ceyaliṉmaiyaik kāṭṭukiṟatu.”

1009. “aṉṟu irāvaṇaṉ cītaiyait tūkkiyatu pōl, caṭṭa maṉṟa uṟuppiṉarkaḷ tūkkappaṭukiṟārkaḷ.” illai, irāvaṇaṉ tūkkiya cītai pativiratai, “caṭṭamaṉṟa uṟuppiṉarkaḷ appaṭiyallavē!”

1010. “makkaḷāṭci naṭaipeṟum nāṭṭil aracu iyantirattai palavīṉappaṭuttak kūṭātu; aracu aluvalarkaḷ matikkappaṭutal vēṇṭum.”

1011. “iṉṟu intiyāvil 90% pērukku vāḻtal kuṟikkōḷ alla. piḻaippu naṭattavē virumpukiṉṟaṉar.”

1012. “taṭaikaḷaik kaṭantum toḻil ceyyum uṇarvu niṟainta uḻaippukkup peyar muyaṟci.”

1013. “iṉṟaiya nāṭṭil aracukku untucaktiyāka viḷaṅkupavarkaḷ viyāpārikaḷē! ataṉāl vilai ēṟukiṟatu.”

1014. “vāḻnāḷ muḻutum paṭittal vēṇṭum, iṭaiyīṭiṉṟip paṭittal vēṇṭum.”

1015. “poṟuppai ēṟkat tayaṅkukiṟavarkaḷ yātoru paṇikkum payaṉpaṭamāṭṭārkaḷ.”

1016. “oḻuṅkamaivu peṟṟa vāḻkkai veṟṟi.”

1017. “camūka nalaṉukkākat taṉṉai aḻittuk koḷkiṟavarkaḷ pākkiyavāṉkaḷ.”

1018. “kacappuṇarcciyum kāḻppuṇarcciyum illāta camutāyam kāṇum muyaṟci tēvai.

1019. “vilai pōkiṟavarkaḷ uḷḷa varai turōkam oru toṭarkatai.”

1020. “keṭṭikkārat taṉattil nūṟu kāriyam ceyvataiviṭa oru nalla kāriyam iyalpākac ceyvatu perumaikkuriyatu.”

1021. “tamiḻiṉa varalāṟṟil iṉa orumaip pāṭṭukkāka vāḻntavar pēraṟiñar aṇṇā oruvar tāṉ.”

1022. maṟṟavarkaḷaip pukaḻumpoḻutu taṉṉaiyum iṇaittuk koḷpavarkaḷ, maṟṟavarkaḷ pukaḻa vāḻa iyalātu.

1023. “oru talaivaṉukkuc ciṟanta niṉaivu avartam koḷkaikaḷai naṭaimuṟaippaṭuttuvatutāṉ.”

1024. “nōy iyaṟkaiyaṉṟu; ceyaṟkaiyē.”

1025. “tīya eṇṇaṅkaḷ kālappōkkil uṭaliṉ ceṅkurutiyaiyē naccut taṉmai aṭaiyac ceytuviṭukiṟatu.”

1026. “aṉpiṉāl niṟainta itayam uḷḷavarkaḷ, cirittu makiḻntu vāḻvārkaḷ."

1027. “kaṭavuḷai nampāmaiyē oru pāvamaṉṟu. maṟṟavarkaḷaip paṟṟi kavalaippaṭāta ācaikaḷē pāvattiṉ pāṟpaṭṭavaiyākum.”

1028. “malarukkuk kēṭu ceyyāmal, tēṉi tēṉai eṭukkiṟatu. atupōlac celvattai īṭṭatal vēṇṭum.”

1029. “matam, toḻilākavum piḻaippākavum māṟiyataṉ viḷaivu nāstikam.”

1030. “nāḷtōṟum vāḻpavarkaḷ eḷitil mutumaiyaṭaivatillai. palar nēṟṟu maṭṭumē vāḻntavarkaḷ.”

1031. “nalla cuṟṟam amaital, pukaḻ mikka vāḻvukku aṭippaṭai.”

1032. “orāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku kūṭat tamiḻ marapukaḷ pēṇamuṭiyāta aḷavukkup palavīṉap paṭukiṉṟōm.”

1033. “caṅkaramaṭam, camutāya aḷavil mēvi varuvatu, tamiḻ marapu vaḷarccikkut tuṇai ceyyātu.”

1034. “kītaiyum tirukkuṟaḷum namatu maṟai eṉṟu kūṟuvatu pētaimai.”

1035. “makkaḷ, ilakkiyam, kalai, icaiivaṟṟai aṉupavikkak kaṟṟuk koṇṭāl nallavarkaḷ ākiviṭuvārkaḷ.”

1036. “kuṭumpa nalat tiṭṭattiṟku celavaḻikkum tokaiyai, kalai vaḷarccikkuc celavaḻittāl nalla kuṭumpam iyalpākavē amaiyum.

1037. “vaitīka pārppaṉiya camayattiṉar tam koḷkaikku māṟākiya tēvāraṅkaḷai mūṭi maṟaittu viṭṭaṉar.”

1038. “irācarācaṉ tirumuṟaikaḷai mīṭṭatiṉ mūlam tamiḻ nākarikattaiyē mīṭṭuk kāttāṉ.”

1039. “tājmakāl, kātali niṉaivil tōṉṟiyatu. tañcaip periya kōvil mātēvaṉ niṉaivil tōṉṟiyatu.”

1040. “ceytip parimāṟṟaṅkaḷ aṟivai vaḷarkkum, nalleṇṇattait tōṟṟuvikkum; iḻappukkaḷ varātu taṭuttuviṭum.”

1041. “tiṭṭamiṭutal eṉpatu kālam, poruḷ, āṟṟalai muṟaippaṭutti vīṇākāmal tavirkkavē.”

1042. “muṉṉē pukaḻavēṇṭum eṉṟu virumpukiṟavarkaḷ, uṇmaiyil pukaḻukkuriyavarkaḷ allar.”

1043. “celvattait toṭarcciyāka īṭṭum pāṅkil nirvakippatē nalla niti nirvākam.”

1044. “tuḻnilaikūṭat taṟcārpait tōṟṟu vikkum. cūḻnilaiyaic cārnta taṟcārpu tavirkka iyalātatu.”

1045. “maṟṟavarkaḷuṭaiya palavīṉam, taṉatu palam eṉṟu niṉaippatu tavaṟu.”

1046. “tamiḻaracarkaḷ āṭciyamaippai valimaippaṭutta, kaṭavuḷ nampikkaiyai makkaḷiṭaiyē parappiṉārkaḷ.”

1047. “nukarum poruḷkaḷāka, ūtiyam vaḻaṅkappeṟṟāl uṟpattip perukum; paṇavīkkamum kuṟaiyum.”

1048. “celvam, celvākku iraṇṭum ērāḷamāṉa naṇparkaḷaik kūṭṭit tarum. itaṉāl payaṉillai. tuṉpattil tuṇaiyāy amaivōrē naṇparkaḷ.”

1049. “poṟuppuṭaṉ paṇiyiṉai ēṟkātavariṭam paṇikaḷai oppaṭaippatu taṟkolaikkuc camam.”

1050. “piṟar muyaṉṟu peṟṟa paṭippiṉaiyiṉ vaḻi, pāṭam kaṟṟuk koḷpavar eṇṇikkaiyē mikuti.”

1051. “poruḷātāra ēḻmaikkukkut tāy uḻaippiṉmaiyē!”

1052. “nalvāḻkkaiyiṉ tēvaikaḷaikkūṭat tiṭṭamiṭṭu aṭaiya muyaṟcippār illai.”

1053. “ellārukkum conta vīṭu kaṭṭik koḷḷa ācaiyirukkiṟatu. āṉāl poruḷ īṭṭum ārvamillai!”

1054. “kaṭanta kālam kaṭantatē. kavalaippaṭṭu payaṉ illai. iṉivarum etirkālam namatu kaiyil illai. ataip paṟṟik kavalaippaṭuvatu poruḷaṟṟatu. ātalāl namatu vacattil irukkiṟa nikaḻkālattaip paṟṟik kavalaippaṭuka.”

1055. “makkaḷukkup poruḷiṉmēl ācaiyirukkiṟatu. āṉāl, uḻaippiṉ mēl ācai illai.”

1056. “entac cūḻnilaiyilum nampikkaiyai iḻantuviṭakkūṭātu.”

1057. “camūka mēmpāṭṭup paṇikaḷai mēṟkoḷpavarkaḷ tīvira kaṭci cārpukaḷai mēṟkoḷḷutal kūṭātu.”

1058. “talaivar kāmarācar avarkaḷ tōṟṟa 1967kkup piṟakē nām araciyal kaṭcip paṇikaḷil īṭupaṭavillai. pārāṭṭutalukkuriya ṭākṭar kalaiñar nammai mēlcapai uṟuppiṉarāka niyamaṉam ceyvatil ārvam kāṭṭi utaviyapōtu kūṭa, araciyal paṇikku aḻaittārē tavira, kaṭci araciyalukku īṭupaṭutta virumpavillai.”

1059. “kuṟikkōḷ vaḻippaṭṭa ceyaṟpāṭukaḷ āḻappaṭumpoḻutu akalam kuṟaikiṟatu. itu viruppu, veṟuppiṉ pāṟpaṭṭatalla. ituvē iṉṟaiya namatu nilai.”

1060. “putiyaṉa kāṇum ārvamē vaḷarccikku vittu.”

1061. “taṉṉaic cērntārait tirutta iyalāmal maṟṟavarkaḷ mītu ciṉamuṟutal tavaṟu. ceyalāṇmaiyaṟṟa nilai.”

1062. “makkaḷai vaḷarccikku āyattappaṭuttutal mutaṟpaṇi.”

1063. “makkaḷiṉ aṟiyāmaiyai etir nōkkil tiruttutal iyalātu. avarkaḷ makiḻum uṇavu, uṭai, mutaliyaṉa vaḻaṅkiṭum uṭaṉpāṭṭup paṇikaḷ mūlamtāṉ iyalum.”

1064. “taṉṉai oḻuṅkuppaṭuttik koḷvatil veṟṟiyiṉ irakaciyam potintuḷḷatu.”

ta-8

1065. “āravāramāka pēcuvatu” - cuṟucuṟuppāṉa vāḻkkai eṉṟu karutuvatu tavaṟu. ceyaṟpāṭṭil tāṉ, cōmpaliṉmaiyaik kāṇa iyalum.”

1066. “oṉṟai, koḷkaiyeṉa vivātattiṟku vaippatē tavaṟu eṉṟāl, karuttu vaḷarcci ēṟpaṭātu.”

1067. “kuṟukiya vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ vāḻpavarkaḷ nallavarkaḷātal aritu.”

1068. “tāṉ koṇṭa muṭivukaḷaiyē vaṟpuṟutta niṉaippavarkaḷiṉ cevip pulaṉ vēlai ceyyātu.”

1069. “kūṭṭu vāḻkkai ciṟappuṟa, maṟṟavar colluvatait taṭaiyiṉṟik kēṭka vēṇṭum eṉṟa nōkkilēyē taṭaiyilāc cevikaḷ amaintuḷḷaṉa.”

1070. “cila kūṟutalum pala kēṭṭalum vāḻkkaikku nallatu.

1071. “potuvāka māṉuṭac cātiyiṉ aṟivārnta poṟikaḷ pulaṉkaḷ aṟivārnta nilaiyil iyaṅkāmal nukarvu nilaiyilēyē iyaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.”

1072. “cintittal, āyvu ceytal, kūṟutal mutaliyaṉa viraintu nikaḻavēṇṭum eṉpataṉālēyē uṭalamaippil aṟivup poṟikaḷ. nīṇṭa iṭaiveḷi ivaikaḷukkiṭaiyil illai.”

1073. “vayiṟum pāluṟuppukkaḷum iyaṅkum aḷavukku mūḷai iyakkam peṟātatu maṉita kulattiṉ kuṟaiyē."

1074. “taṭpa veṭpa nilaikaḷ uṭal nalamuṭai yōrukkut tīṅku ceyvatillai.”

1075. “piriviṉaikaḷum vēṟṟumaikaḷum uḷḷa varaiyil, maṉitakulam amaitiyāka vāḻaiyalātu.”

1076. “kuṟukiya pātaikaḷ payaṇattiṟku ēṟpuṭaiyaṉa ākā! atupōlak kuṟukiya nōkkaṅkaḷ vāḻkkaip payaṇattiṟkup payaṉtarā.”

1077. “urimaiyiyal caṭṭam aṉaivarukkum oṉṟātal nallatu.”

1078. “intu camutāyattil nilavum piriviṉaikaḷ vaḻi, cilar ākkam aṭaikiṉṟaṉar. ataṉāl, oṉṟu paṭuttum paṇi eḷitaṉṟu.”

1079. “namatu camutāyak kaṭṭamaippil astivāram cantēkam.”

1080. “makaḷir, taṅkaḷuṭaiya vāḻkkaiyil peṟaṟkariyatu urimai eṉṟu karutiviṭṭāl viṭutalai kiṭaittuviṭum.”

1081. “kalvit tiṟaṉum poṟuppum uṭaiya māṇavarkaḷ paḷḷikku varāmalē kaṟṟuk koṇṭu viṭuvar.”

1082. “cuṟṟuppuṟac cūḻnilai cariyillai eṉṟu kūṟukiṟavarkaḷ pērāṭṭa uṇarvillāmal piḻaippu naṭattupavarkaḷ.”

1083. “etaṟkum camātāṉam collamuṭiyum. āṉāl, ēṟṟukkoḷḷa iyalātu.”

1084. “iṉṟaiya āciriyarkaḷaippōla mūḷaic cōmpēṟikaḷ eṉṟum iruntatillai,”

1085. “oru ceyalaik kaṭamai uṇarvuṭaṉ ceytāl atu aḻakākavum payaṉtarat takkatākavum amaiyum.”

1086. “eḷitil celvam kiṭaittāl uyir vaḷarātu; takuti: peṟātu.”

1087. “tuṉpam niṟainta ceyaṟpāṭukaḷē payaṉum paṇpāṭum niṟainta vāḻkkaikku aṭippaṭai.”

1088. “muḻuk kavaṉattuṭaṉ ceyyap peṟāta paṇikaḷ uriya makiḻvait taruvatillai.”

1089. “ovvoruvarum avaravarkaḷukkuk kiṭaitta vāyppukaḷai naḻuvaviṭāmal payaṉpaṭuttik koṇṭāl muṉṉēṟalām.”

1090. “tēṭum paḻakkam aṟivai vaḷarkkum. āḷviṉai āṟṟalait tarum.”

1091. “celvattaic celavukkāka eṉṟu tēṭutal kūṭātu. iruppukku eṉṟu tēṭutal vēṇṭum.”

1092. “keṭṭa maṉitarkaḷ erimalai pōla uḷ pukaintu koṇṭē iruppar.”

1093. “uṭal, uyir, aṟivu mūṉṟum camanilaiyil vaḷarntālē maṉitakulam mēmpāṭu aṭaiya muṭiyum.”

1094. “ulakil vaṟumaikku kāraṇam uḻaippiṉmaiyum uḻaippukku uriya pāṅkiṉmaiyumē yākum.”

1095. “uttaravātamuḷḷa vāḻkkaiyilēyē kātal ciṟakkum.”

1996. “maṟṟavarkaḷiṭam poṟuppuk koṭuppatu eḷitu. āṉāl poṟuppu ēṟpatē muṟai.”

1097. “cāmpirāṇip pukaiyai nukarntāl kēṭu: pukaiyiṉ maṉattai maṭṭum nukarntāl nalam.”

1098. “pakuttaṟivu, pēccup poruḷ alla; atu oru vāḻkkai muṟai.”

1099. “eppōtum uḻaippukkuriya āyatta nilaiyil iruppatē-viḻittiruttal.”

1100. “pala nāḷ uḻāta nilam pāṟaiyākiṟatu. palanāḷ aṟivāl uḻappaṭāta maṉitar pulaṉkaḷ kallākiviṭukiṉṟaṉa.”





1101. “caṭṭattai eḷitil vimarcaṉam ceyyalām; āṉāl ēṟṟu oḻukutal kaṭiṉam.”

1102. “caṭṭaṅkaṉ naṉmaiyai vaḷarkkum taṉmai uṭaiyaṉa alla; tīmaiyai taṭukkum nōkka muṭaiyaṉavēyām.”

1103. “pūṭṭukkaḷai nampukiṟavarkaḷ. maṟṟavarkaḷ yōkkiyat taṉmaiyai nampuvatāka naṭippatu ēṉ?”

1104. “aṉpum, aṟaṉum kalavāta vāḻkkaiyil piṟakkum kuḻantaikaḷai vaḷarkka, ciṟanta paḷḷikaḷum-ūrum tēvai.”?

1105. “ācaippaṭukiṉṟaṉar! āṉāl, āḷumaikku añcukiṉṟaṉar.”

1106. “uḻaittu vāḻāta vāḻkkai, vipacāram.”

1107. “uṭal uṟuppukkaḷil oṉṟu kuṟaintālum caṅkaṭappaṭupavarkaḷ, tirunta vēlai ceykiṟārkaḷ illaiyē!”

1108. “poruḷātāra ātipattiyap pōṭṭikaḷ ulakan taḻuviyatāka vaḷarntu varukiṉṟatu.”

1109. “cārntē vāḻpavarkaḷ, naṭunilaiyāḷarkaḷai vāḻa aṉumatikka vēṇṭum.”

1110. “intiya araciyal vātikaḷ ātāyam tēṭum maṉappōkku, cōram pōkum nilai ākiyavaṟṟiṉ kāraṇamāka intiyāviṟku jaṉātipati āṭci muṟai tēvaiyākiviṭum.”

1111. “cātāraṇa payaṉpaṭāta kuṭicaiyaik kūṭa yārum iḻakkat tayārāka illai.”

1112. “poṟikaḷai, iṉpa nukarvil pakkuvappaṭuttuvatu kalai.”

1113. “evvaḷavu tiṟamaiyākak kāriyam cey tālum toṭarcci illaiyāṉāl payaṉ illai.”

1114. “kālamaṟintu ceyyāta kaṭamaikaḷ uriya payaṉai tārā.”

1115. “oru karuttil ūṉṟātavarkaḷ cuṟṟic cuṟṟi varuvar.”

1116. “iṟaivaṉ vāḻttup poruḷ alla. vāḻvup poruḷ.”

1117. “iṉṟu vāḻkiṉṟavarkaḷ, varalāṟṟup pōkkil vantamainta vāḻkkai. amaippukkaḷai aṭutta talaimuṟaiyiṉarukku appaṭiyē viṭṭu vaittal kaṭaṉākum.”

1118. “caivattiṟku ciṟappu cērkka nālvar vaḻipāṭu tēvai.”

1119. “pakaimai pārāṭṭāmal vāḻtal oru ciṟanta paṇpu.”

1120. “talaimaikku ācaippaṭātu maṟṟavarkkut talaimaiyai viṭṭuttarum tairiyacālikaḷ āyiram āṇṭukku oru taṭavaitāṉ piṟakkiṉṟaṉar.”

1121. “aṉṟāṭam kaṇakkup pārkkātavaṉ ākkam peṟa iyalātu.”

1122. “kālam eṉṟa kaḷattiṉ arumaiyai iṉṉamum palar aṟiyātatu, intiya camūka vāḻkkaiyiṉ tīmaikaḷuḷ oṉṟu.”

1123. “aṭaiyavēṇṭum eṉṟa muṉaippuṭaṉ kūṭiyatē muyaṟci.”

1124. “kāriyat tēvaikaḷ, maṉitaṉai nirvāṉa nilaikkut taḷḷiviṭukiṉṟaṉa.”

1125. “mamataiyiṉ muṉṉāl ciṟu tavaṟukaḷum periyaṉavākat tōṉṟum.”

1126. “nām ākki vaittāl pōtātu. ūṭṭi viṭavēṇṭum eṉṟum cilar etirpārkkiṟārkaḷ.”

1127. “cintaiyil akantai uṭaiyavarkaḷ eḷitil aṇukamāṭṭārkaḷ.”

1128. “muntirip paḻattil koṭṭai veḷiyē turuttik koṇṭiruppatāl paḻattiṟkuc cuvai kuṟaivu.”

1129. “mariyātai’ eṉṟa peyarāl viḷaiyum maṉaccaṅkaṭaṅkaḷē mikuti.”

1130. “maṉa eḻucciyuṭaṉ pēcupavarkaḷ nilaittu niṟkamāṭṭārkaḷ.”

1131. “coṉṉataic coṉṉapaṭi niṟaivēṟṟu pavarkaḷē uḻaippāḷikaḷ.”

1132. oḻuṅkumuṟai ākkattiṟku aṭippaṭai.”

1133. “piracava vētaṉai illāmal tāy illai. atupōla tuṉpappāṭu illāmal iṉpam illai.”

1134. periya vēlaikaḷ naṭaipeṟat taṭaiyāyiruppavai, ciṉṉa ciṉṉa vēlaikaḷ muṟaiyākac ceyyap peṟāmaiyē!”

1135. tāyiṭam aṉpu pārāṭṭuvatē oru kalai tāṉ.”

1136. “koḷkaip piṭivātam vāḻkkaiyai vaṟaṭcit taṉmaiyuṭaiyatākkiviṭum.”

1137. “illāmaiyāl ulaku aṭaiyum tuṉpattai viṭa ācaiyāl paṭum tuṉpamē mikuti.”

1138. “uyiruṭaṉ koṇṭu cellum aṟivu, paṇpukaḷil uḷḷa ārvattaiviṭa uṭaṟcārpāṉa ācaikaḷē ulakattai āṭṭippaṭaikkiṉṟaṉa.”

1139. vēlaiyil īṭupāṭu, kuṟikkōḷ iruntālē varum.”

1140. oru kālattil naṉmaiyākak karutap peṟuvatu nilaitta naṉmaiyāki viṭuvatillai.”

1141. “eṉakku naṇparāka iruntāl pōtātu. eṉatu pakaivarukku virōtiyākavum iruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu etirpārppatu virumpattakka talla.”

1142. “uṇarcci niṟainta vēkattil irukkum poḻutu aṟaneṟi upatēcam payaṉpaṭātu. anta nilaiyil marunteṉa uṇarccikku irai pōṭutal payaṉtarum. āṉāl āpattāṉa paricōtaṉai.”

1143. “uṭaṟpayiṟci kāraṇamākavē palar vēlai ceyya niṉaikkiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷukku uḻaippu nōkkam alla.”

1144. “etaiyum piṉ toṭarcciyākak kavaṉikkat tavaṟiṉāl keṭum.”

1145. “niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷ, iyakkanilai eytutal vēṇṭum.”

1146. “uṭal ōyātu iyaṅkukiṟatu. atupōla vāḻkkai ōyātu iyaṅkutal vēṇṭum.”

1147. iyaṟkai amaittut tarum kaṉikaḷilum maṉitaṉ camaikkum uṇavu nallaṉa alla.”

1148. “uṇmaiyāka naṭattal eṉpatu akamum puṟamum ottu naṭattal eṉpatē!”

1149. “intiya camūkap piriviṉaikaḷ, tīmaikaḷ nīṅkiyē yākavēṇṭum.”

1150. “kuṟikkōḷai aṭaiyum vāḻkkaikkut tuṉpattait tuṇaiyākak koḷḷum tuṇivu tēvai.”

1151. “namatu makkaḷāṭcimuṟai maṉu eḻutum paḻakkattaiyē kaṟṟut tantuḷḷatu.”

1152. “uḷam niṟai īṭupāṭē uḻaippukku ūṟṟu.”

1153. “kārl mārks matattai etirkkum poḻutu, anta iṭattil oru putiya matattai vāḻkkai muṟaiyai aṟimukappaṭuttiṉār. ataṉāl oraḷavu veṟṟi peṟṟār. āṉāl, periyār etirppai maṭṭumē ceytār. ataṉāl nilaittu niṟkavillai. avar pācaṟaiyil vaḷarntavarkaḷēkūṭa kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭṭukku vantuviṭṭaṉar. atu maṭṭumalla. matattai etirttup pēcutal aṟiyāmaiyiṉ ceyal eṉṟu eṇṇukiṉṟaṉar.

1154. “camayam, camūkam, iraṇṭum iṇainta nōkkaṅkaḷē! inta iṇaippu parāmarikkap peṟṟālē camutāyam vaḷarum”

1155. “intiya araciyal paṭumōcamāṉa nilaikkuppōyk koṇṭirukkiṟatu. veṟi nāy nilaikku vantuviṭṭatu.

1156. “enta oṉṟilum iṟukap paṟṟiya cārpu vantuviṭṭāl nīti pulappaṭātu.”

1157. “ulaka māṉiṭa cāti ‘cutantira’ varalāṟṟai uṇarntālē cutantarattai iḻakkat tuṇiyamāṭṭārkaḷ.”

1158. “ceyalaṟṟavarkaḷ, ceyaluḷḷavarkaḷ mītu aḻukkāṟu koḷḷutal tavirkka iyalātatu, kavalaṟka.”

1159. “āḷumai eṉpatu oru kalai. atai nāḷtōṟum payilutal vēṇṭum.”

1160. “ceyaṟpāṭṭukkuriya āyatta nilaiyil irukkap paḻakiyavarkaḷ ciṟanta āḷumaiyaip peṟuvārkaḷ.”

1161. “aṟiviyal, poruḷiyal, āṭciyiyal, aruḷiyal aṉaittum vīṭṭu vācaṟpaṭikku vantālum ēṟka muṉvarā cila apūrvap piṟavikaḷ uṇṭu.”

1162. “kāmam-aṟivārnta iṉpam varavēṟkattakkatu. kāmam kaḷiyāṭṭamāyiṉ taram kuṟaiyum.”

1163. “oru kuḻantaikkum peṟṟōrātal peṟaṟ kariya pēṟu.”

1164. “maṉaveḻucci aṭaṅkiya nilaiyē pūcakar ātalukkut takuti.”

1165. “tuḻnilaikaḷai etirttup pōrāṭuvatē uytikku vaḻi.”

1166. “ulakantaḻiiya oṭpam peṟutalē iṉṟu mutaṟkaṭamai.(oṭpam-mēṉmai)

1167. “naṉmaikaḷukkellām naṉmai aḻukkā ṟiṉmaiyē.”

1168. “kāmam’-uyirkaḷukku viruppam niṟainta vāḻkkaiyait tūṇṭuvatu.”

1169. “tuyttal pāpam illai. tuykkum pāṅkiyal tāṉ puṇṇiya, pāpam karuk koḷkiṟatu.”

1170. “ceyyum ceyalkaḷ payaṉ taruvatillai. nōkkaṅkaḷē payaṉait taravallaṉa.”

1171. “vaṉtaṉmai’ - koḷḷutal vāḻkkai veṟṟikaḷaik keṭukkum.”

1172. “tiruttamuṟac ceypavarkaḷ kiṭaikkātu pōṉāl nāmē ceytuviṭuvatu nallatu.”

1173. “yār iṟantāl eṉṉa? caccaravu niṟkumā?”

1174. “kuṭumpa uṟavukaḷ ceḻittu vaḷarvatē nākarikattiṉ aṭippaṭai,”

1175. uyirkaḷ maṉa vikāramiṉṟic ceḻittu vaḷartalē nākarikattiṉ aṭippaṭai.”

1176. “cuḻalum irāṭṭaiyākiviṭṭatu iṉṟaiya māṉuṭa vāḻkkai.”

1177. “kaṭci jaṉanāyakam illaiyāṉāl āṭciyil amarpavarkaḷ keṭṭup pōvārkaḷ.”

1178. “uṇarvu pūrvamāka oṉṟaic ceyya muṉvarātavarkaḷiṭam paṇiyai oppaṭaippatu tavaṟu.”

1179. “iṉṟaiya iḷaiñarkaḷ ācaippaṭukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl aṟivaṟinta āḷviṉaiyil viruppamillai.”

1180. “uḻaippāl āḷa ulakattil uḻaippiṉai vilaiyākat tarāmal vāḻa niṉaippatu kuṟṟam.”

1181. “kuṟaivākac ceytālum muṟaiyum oḻuṅkum taḻuviya ceyalmuṟai tēvai.”

1182. “paṇam tēvaiyirukkiṟatu. paṉam varum vāyilum irukkiṟatu. āṉāl, eṭuppāraittāṉ kāṇōm.”

1183. “payiṟci peṟāta uḻaippāḷikaḷ ceyvārkaḷ. āṉāl pōtiya payaṉ irukkātu.”

1184. “kūṭṭattaik kuṟai! uḻaippiṉ tarattai uyarttuka.”

1185. “vitimuṟaikaḷ, ceṉṟa kālap paṭippiṉaikaḷ īṉṟa kuḻantaikaḷēyām.”

1186. “taṟkāppu uṇarvu” muṉaippāka iruppavarkaḷ kalantu pēca maṟuppārkaḷ.”

1187. “enta oṉṟaiyum tīra āyvu ceyyāmal-ceyvatu iḻappai tarum.”

1188. “nirvākap pōkkil āyvu ceyvatu-vaḷarccikkut tuṇai ceyya inta āyvukku ‘nampikkai’ eṉṟa tattuvam taṭaiyāka amaital kūṭātu.”

1189. “oru paṇiyil oruvarai toṭarntu īṭupaṭuttutal-kēṭṭukku vaḻi vakukkum.”

1190. “taṭai illāta vaṇṭiyum āyvu illāta nirvākamum vipattukkaḷait tōṟṟu vikkum.”

1191. “ācai-āttiram takutikaḷiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil tōṉṟuvatillai.”

1192. “iṉam - moḻiyiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil tōṉṟi nilavum vaḻakku-itu ulaka vaḻakku cāti mata aṭippaṭaiyil, iṉam iruttal iyalātu, kūṭātu”

1193. “cāti’ piṟappiṉpāṟpaṭṭatu. itu ayal vaḻakku: tamiḻ vaḻakkaṉṟu. nītikkum vaḷarccikkum puṟampāṉatu.”

1194. “kulam’ vaḻi vaḻi varum marapu. itu ēṟpuṭaiyatu. marapu vaḻiyum cūḻnilaiyiṉ tākkamum tavirkka iyalātaṉa.”

1195. matam-maṭam ākiya amaippukkaḷ appaṭṭamāṉa cātiyamaippukkaḷē! iṅkellām putumaiyai etirpārttal iyalātu.

I196. “mēyppār illāta māṭupōla kaṇkāṇippu illāta nirvākak keṭum.”

1197. “oruvariṭamē pala kālattukku orē paṇi iruppatu naṟpaḻakkattait tūṇṭuvatillai.”

1198. “teḷivākak karuttup parimāṟṟam illaiyāṉāl kāriyak kēṭuvarum.”

1199. “paṭittavarkaḷiṭam uḷḷa tīyap paṇpukaḷ akaṉṟālē-camutāyam uruppaṭṭuviṭum.”

1200. “maṟṟavarkaḷiṉ pakti nammuṭaiya peṭṭiyait tīṇṭa aṉumatikkakkūṭātu.”





1201. “maṟṟavar cevvi pārttu oḻukutal uyariyap paṇpu.”

1202. “tērtalaiyē nōkkamākak koṇṭa araciyal, makkaḷaip piccaik kārarākkiviṭum.”

1203. “aracu tarmaccālaiyāka māṟiviṭak kūṭātu.”

1204. “ellām iruntum kāriyam naṭakka villai. ēṉ? itutāṉ maṉitariṉ viparītamāṉa pōkku.”

1205. “palanāḷ paḻakiyavarkaḷ paṇpil talaip piriyamāṭṭārkaḷ.”

1206. “iṉṟaiya āḷuṅkaṭciyiṉariṉ aṭittaḷattiṉar nāṭē taṅkaḷuṭaiyatu eṉṟa muṭivukku vantu āṭṭam pōṭukiṉṟaṉar. intap pōkku etir viḷaivukaḷai uruvākkuvatu iyaṟkaiyiṉ niyati! tavirkka iyalātatu.”

1207. “tāḻttappaṭṭōr, piṟpaṭṭōr, muṟpaṭṭōr paṭṭiyal oru nāṭṭil nilaiyāka iruppiṉ atu nītikkum vaḷarcci eṉṟa niyatikkum māṟupaṭṭa camutāya amaippākum.”

1208. “matippu, matiyātai eṉpatu vilai kūṭutalukkuriyatu eṉṟa nilai poymmaiyai vaḷarkkum”

1209. “kālam, uḻaippu, payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum poruḷkaḷ ivaṟṟiṉ kūṭṭu matippukkuriyaṉa. tirumpak kiṭaikkāta etuvum iḻappaiyē tarum.”

1210. “vāḻkkaiyum oruvakai viyāpāramē. vāḻtalukkuriya celavai viṭa aṭaital kūṭutalāka vēṇṭum. ituvē ūtiyam niṟainta vāḻkkai.”

1211. “paṇpāṭillātavar atikāram peṟutal, kuraṅku kaiyil koḷḷi peṟṟatu pōlākum.”

1212. “nirvākam eṉpatu kālam, iṭam, poruḷ, uḻaippu ivaṟṟai muṟaippaṭuttip payaṉ paṭuttip payaṉ koḷḷutalākum.”

1213. “kōppukaḷ kuṟikkōḷuṭaiyaṉavāka iyakkap peṟātu pōṉāl iḻappēyām.”

1214. “kaṇṇāl kāṇpatu, kātāl kēṭpatu ākiyaṉa mūlam toṭarcciyāṉa ceyalūkkattaip peṟātavarkaḷ iruntālum iṟantavarkaḷēyāvar.”

1215. “cariyāṉa nērattil cariyāṉa takavalait tarutal nirvākattiṟku valimaiyaic cērkkum. ākkattiṉait tarum; iḻappukkaḷait tavirkkum.”

1216. “mukkāli mūṉṟukāluṭaiyatu. itil orukāl uṭaintālum payaṉ illai. atupōlak kūṭṭamaippil taṉi oruvariṉ palavīṉam kūṭa pātippukaḷait tarum.”

1217. “aṟiviyal, uḻaippai eḷimaip paṭuttik koṇṭu varukiṟatu. āṉāl vāḻkkaiyai arumaippaṭuttik koṇṭu vrukiṟatu.”

1218. “mākkam, viyappu ākiyavaṟṟiṟkuriyaṉa vāḻkkaiyai pūraṇattuvam ceyyā.”

1219. “urimai uṇarvē nilaiyāṉa uṟavukku aṭippaṭai.”

1220. “oruvar veṟukkappaṭutalukkuk kāraṇam avartam iyalpukaḷ maṭṭumalla. maṟṟavarkaḷ eṭukkum muṭivukaḷum kāraṇam ākum.”

1221. “ovvoruvarum avaravar vāḻkkaiyai muṟaippaṭuttik koṇṭāl yārukkum iṭarppāṭu illai.”

1222. “oru camūka māṟṟam eḷitil nikaḻātu. toṭarcciyāṉa pōrāṭṭam tēvai.”

1223. “enta oru tīmaiyum toṭakkattil tayakkattuṭaṉ talaikāṭṭukiṟatu. piṉ paḻakkattiṉ kāraṇamākat tīmaiyē iyalpākiviṭukiṟatu.”

1224. “cāmārttiyamum tiṟamaiyum oṉṟalla.”

1225. “cāmārttiyam eppaṭiyum kāriyattai cātippatu. tiṟamai, muṟaippaṭi cātippatu,”

1226. “appāvikaḷ nallavarkaḷallar. tīmai ceyya caktiyaṟṟavarkaḷ.”

1227. “nallavar'-itu muḻu niṟaic col. āṟṟaluṭaiyavarkaḷē nallavarkaḷāka vāḻtal iyalum.”

1228. “māṉam illātavarkaḷ enta tavaṟukaḷukkum varuntamāṭṭārkaḷ. tiruntavum māṭṭārkaḷ.”

1229. “aṭikkaṭi ēṟpaṭum celavukaḷil viḻippāka iruppatu niti nirvākattiṟkut tēvai."

1230. “eṇṇey tēyttuk koṇṭu piṉ cīyakkāy koṇṭu kaḻuvi viṭuvatu pōl, tuyppait tuṟavāl kaḻuvavēṇṭum.”

1231. “cittāṭukiṟavarkaḷ nām virumpuvataik koṭuttāl nampalām.”

1232. “ūkkam eṉpatu ceyalūkkaṅkaḷēyām.”

1233. “oṉṟaiyoṉṟu piriyāta nilaiyil piṇaikkappeṟṟa piṇaippē vāḻvaḷikkiṉṟaṉa. atupōla camūkam piṇaikkap peṟṟāl tāṉ vāḻvu ciṟakkum.”

1234. “tavaṟukaḷ ceyvatum viḷaiyāṭṭāki viṭṭa viparītam vaḷarntu varukiṟatu.”

1235. “poṟuppu uṇarvu kaṭaiccarakkaṉṟu. vāḻviṉ paṭippukaḷāl peṟuvatu.”

1236. “uyarvaṟa uyarum nilaiyē nilai. ituvē varavēṟkattakkatu.”

1237. “paṭippaṟivilum paṭṭaṟivu ciṟantatu.”

1238. “kāytal-kuḷirtal eṉṟa niyatikaḷai poruḷkaḷai māṟṟukiṉṟaṉa. ātalāl, kāytalum kuḷirtalum vāḻkkaiyai vaḷarppaṉa.”

1239. “oḻuṅkāka amaikkap peṟṟa paṟkaḷē uṇavai aṟaikka utavukiṉṟaṉa. atupōla oḻuṅkamaivupaṭṭa uḻaippē uṇarvukaḷai vaḷarkkum; ākkattiṉai nalkum.

1240. “uṭalamaivil mutaṉmai, iraṇṭām nilai eṉṟa nilai enta uṟuppukkum illai.

ta-9 etuvum cariyāka iyaṅkātupōṉāl talaiyum keṭum. ātalāl, ovvoṉṟum muṟaiyāka iyaṅkutalē vēṇṭum.”

1241. “uyirppuḷḷa viḻippu nilaiyil camutāyam iyaṅkiṉāl vāḻnilai ciṟakkum.”

1242. “peṇ, oru pōkap poruḷ eṉṟa karuttu uḷḷavaraiyil peṇṇaṭimaittaṉam nīṅkātu.”

1243. “cuṟṟuppuṟac cūḻnilai kāraṇamāka nallavarkaḷāka iruppatum iyaṟkai.”

1244. “tamiḻarkaḷ uyar cintaṉaiyāḷarkaḷ; āṉāl ceyalāḷarkaḷ allar.”

1245. “niṟaiya īṭṭal; kuṟaivākac celavaḻittal-ituvē nalvāḻkkaiyiṉ niyati.”

1246. “celvam kiṭaikkum poḻutu nukarvaik kūṭṭik koṇṭāl, varuvāy kuṟaiyumpōtu iṭarppāṭu tōṉṟum.”

1247. “kaṭamaikaḷ, urimaikaḷ, poṟuppuṇarvukaḷ ākiyaṉa icainta oru paṇpē, kūṭṭuṟavu eṉpatu.”

1248. “ciṟanta kūṭṭuṟavāḷaṉ colvataṟku muṉpu kēṭpāṉ; vāṅkuvataṟku muṉpu koṭuppāṉ; uṇpataṟku muṉpu uḻaippāṉ.”

1249. “ākkam tēṭi, muṭiyum eṉṟa nampikkaiyil “peṟamuṭiyātavarkaḷ iḻappil peritum kavalaippaṭuvar.”

1250. “taṉṉaip pōla maṟṟavarkaḷ eṉṟu niṉaikkātavarkaḷ camūkattil vāḻvatu virumpat takkatalla.”

1251. “tēvaiyillāmal pēcuvatu payaṉil col kūṟutalē.”

1252. “kaṟaiyāṉ tāṉāka virumpi muyaṉṟu uḻaittu vīṭu kaṭṭuvatillai. āṉāl kaṭṭiya vīṭṭil eḷitil kuṭi pukukiṟatu.”

1253. “kaṭṭiya vīṭṭiṉ arumai teriyāta kaṟaiyāṉ atai aḻikkiṟatu. atupōla naṉmaiyaṟi yātavarkaḷ naṉmaiyai aḻippārkaḷ”.

1254. “payattiṉiṉum kēṭu piṟitoṉṟu illai.”

1255. “vāyiṉāl muṭikkakkūṭiya kāriyattai eḻuttiṉāl ceyyakkūṭātu.”

1256. “toḻilil iḻappu eṉṟu kūṟi, toḻilāḷarkaḷukku nalla ūtiyam koṭukka maṟuppatu nalla nirvākam alla.”

1257. “nirvākam eṉṟa amaippē, cīruṟa naṭattavē!”

1258. “etaiyum poṟuppuṭaṉ ceyya muṉ varātavarkaḷ, maṟṟavarkaḷ mītu paḻi pōṭuvar.”

1259. “palarōṭu paḻakuvatāl utavikaḷ ceyvatilum koṭaiyaḷippatilumkūṭa cikkal tōṉṟum.”

1260. “ovvoru uṟavum poṟuppukkaḷaiyum kaṭamaikaḷaiyum koṇṭatēyām.”

1261. “uṟavukaḷ aṉaittum etirpārppukaḷai uṭaiyaṉavē. etirpāāppukaḷaic ceyya iyalāta pōtu maṉattāṅkal ēṟpaṭum.”

1262. “ceyyum maṉamiruntāl vāyppukaḷ tāmē vanteytum.”

1263. “pukaḻ-mēlāṇmai-ceyaltiṟaṉ ākiya naṉmaitaru tuṟaikaḷilkūṭa aḻukkāṟu tōṉṟāmal taṭukka, taṉṉaṭakkam tēvai.”

1264. “kuṟainta vilaiyuṭaiya uṇavup paṇṭaṅkaḷē nalla uṇavukkup payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa.”

1265. iyaṟkaiyai oṭṭic ceṉṟāl etuvum ceyyalām. iyaṟkaiyiliruntu māṟupaṭum vāḻ nilai kūṭātu.”

1266. “ceyaṟkai, iyaṟkaiyōṭu poruntātu.”

1267. “nalla camaiyaṟkārar kiṭaittuviṭṭālē āyiram kāriyam ceyyalām.”

1268. “namatu uṭalaik kavaṉikkavē uṟavukaḷ: āṉāl naṭappatutāṉ illai.”

1269. “taṉṉai eṉṟum iḷamaiyuṭaiyōṉākat karutik koḷḷavēṇṭum eṉpataṟkāka-poymmaiyāka uṭaliṉ nilaiyiṉai maṟaittu vāḻtal kūṭātu.”

1270. “uṭaṉukkuṭaṉ nōykkuriya kāraṇattai māṟṟātu vāḻvatu, nōyai atikappaṭuttum.”

1271. “nōyiṉ kāraṇattai māṟṟāmal nōykku maruntuṇpavarkaḷ, nirantara nōyāḷikaḷ ākiviṭuvar.” 1272. “periya maṉitarkaḷukku nōy varuvatē oru cittiravatai! taṇṭaṉai!”



1273. “pala nūṟu tuḷikaḷ cērntatutāṉ āḻkaṭal; kācukaḷākac cērkkap peṟṟāl celvamum kōṭiyāki viṭum.”



1274. “cillaṟai uṟavukaḷ celvattiṟkuk kēṭu.”



1275. “ēḻaikaḷiṭattil uḷḷa aiyappāṭē muṉṉēṟṟattiṟku mutal taṭai.”



1276. “vēṇṭuvār vēṇṭuvatellām iṟaivaṉ vaḻaṅki viṭṭāl, ulakam vicittira vilaṅku kūṭāramākiviṭum. ataṉāltāṉ iṟaivaṉ vaḻaṅkat takkataiyē vaḻaṅkukiṟāṉ!”



1277. “kallil muḷai aṭippatum, kallātavarukku upatēcippatum vīṇ.”



1278. “ācaippaṭupavarkaḷum, nirvāṇa cātukkaḷum veṭkattai viṭṭavarkaḷ.”



1279. “pataviyil cukam irukkiṟa varai pōṭṭikaḷait tavirkka iyalātu.”



1280. “niṟuvaṉattiṟkāka taṅkaḷ vāḻkkaiyai arppaṇikkāta ūḻiyarkaḷaik koṇṭu niṟuvaṉattaik kāppāṟṟa iyalātu.”



1281. "arppaṇippu uṇarvillāta amaippil, mēlāṇmai kalvi ēṟpuṭaiya takkavarāka amaittal vēṇṭum."



1282. “taṉakku ivar utaviyē tīrvār eṉṟa nampikkaiyum kūṭa, vāḻkkaiyil alaṭciyattait tōṟṟuvikkiṟatu”.





1283. “ciṟuciṟu paṇikaḷaik kūṭa muṟaiyākac ceyyātavarkaḷ kai niṟaiya ūtiyam etirpārkkiṉṟaṉar.”

1284. “oru iṉattiṉ oṟṟumai viruppamē iṉṟu aiyappāṭṭiṟku uriyatākiviṭṭatu.”

1285. “meypporuḷ uṇartal eṉpatu vēṟu. kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭu eṉpatu vēṟu.”

1286. “meyññāṉamum matamum oṉṟalla.”

1287. “meyññāṉam kāṇpatuvē vāḻkkaiyiṉ kuṟikkōḷ.”

1288. “viññāṉam curaṇṭum varkkattukkuk kūṭa, tuṇai pōkiṟatu.”

1289. “viññāṉam iyaṟkaiyiṉ uḷḷīṭukaḷaik kaṇṭu vāḻkkaiyiṉ aṉupavattiṟkuk koṇṭu varum ariyap paṇiyaic ceykiṟatu.”

1290. “viññāṉam ēvalaṉāka iruppatu naṉṟu.”

1291. “viññāṉam talaimaiyāka māṟak kūṭātu.”

1292. “oruvaruṭaiya camayam toṭarpāṉa nampikkaikaḷ - avarkaḷuṭaiya vāḻkkaiyaiyum - anta nāṭṭiṉ camūka vāḻvaiyum pātikkac ceyyum.”

1293. “intiya camūka vāḻkkaiyiṉ vaṟumaikku mikutiyum kāraṇam ūḻ tattuvaṅkaḷēyām.”

1294. “varicaippaṭutal eṉṟa marapu nallatu.”

1295. “avaṉ - avaḷuṭaṉ ataṉaip (iyaṟkaiyai) payaṉpaṭutti vāḻtalē- vāḻkkai."

1296. “vallāṅku vāḻtal eḷitu. vāḻvāṅku vāḻtal aritu.”

1297. “vāṉavar ulakam vaḷam niṟaintatu: āṉāl, paṇpāṭaṟṟatu.”

1298. “maṉita vāḻkkaikku mikavum iṉṟiyamaiyātatu paṇpāṭēyām.”

1299. “vāḻkkaikku mikavuyarnta kuṟikkōḷ amaiyāviṭil vāḻkkai ciṟattal iyalātu.”

1300. “maṟṟavar varampu ukanta cutantirattai eṭuttuk koḷvatu vāḻkkaiyaik keṭukkum.”





1301. “tayavilum tavaṟukaḷ nikaḻvatu uṇṭu.”

1302. “aṟivoṭum - naṭaimuṟaiyoṭum poruntāt tayavu naṉṟaṉṟu.”

1303. “ovvoru uyirum kaṭaicit taruvāyilum kūṭa cukittiṭavē virumpum.”

1304. “malaikkup pakkattil niṟpatellām malaiyākiviṭātu. atupōla periya maṉitaraic cērntavarkaḷ ellām periya maṉitarkaḷ illai.”

1305. “oṉṟai ūkkattuṭaṉ ceytāl eḷitil kāriyam muṭiyum.”

1306. “ceymuṟaikaḷuṭaṉ pāṭam pōtikkum marapu vaḻit tiruvuruvamē ālamar kaṭavuḷ.”

1307. “caṭṭaṅkaḷ varaiyaṟaiyil niṟkum amaippukkaḷ vaḻaṅka iyalātu.”

1308. “niti mikamika uyarntatu."

1309. “ovvoru intiyaṉum matac cārpaṟṟa taṉmaiyuṭaiyavaṉāy iruntālē matac cārpaṟṟa nāṭu.”

1310. “poṟuppuṇarnta nilai, vāḻkkaiyai iyakkamākki viṭum.”

1311. “vāḻkkaiyil uḷḷa cōmpalē poṟuppiṉmaikkuk kāraṇam.”

1312. “iṉteṉa vēlai terintiruntum ataic ceyyātu cuṟṟuvatu alaiyum maṉattiṉ aṭaiyāḷam.”

1313. “iṟai vaḻipāṭu maṭṭumē vāḻkkai eṉṟāl māṉuṭap piṟappai iṟaivaṉ ceytirukka vēṇṭiyatillai.”

1314. “araciṉ tiṭṭaṅkaḷai aṟintu koḷḷutalē ākkattiṉ mutaṟpaṭi.”

1315. “celavukaḷil mutaṟ celavu cēmittal”

1316. “koṭiya tuṉpaṅkaḷaiyumkūṭa makiḻvāka aṉupavikkalām-vāḻkkaiyil vaḷaravēṇṭum eṉṟa uṇarviruntāl.”

1317. “camutāya amaippiṉ ciṟpi āciriyarē.”

1318. “ovvoruvarukkum orē oru upācaṉā mūrttitāṉ irukkavēṇṭum. entat tiruvuruvat tiṉaik kaṇṭālum taṉatu upācaṉā mūrttiyiṉ vaṇṇamāka niṉaippatē marapu.”

1319. “maṉacāṭci” eṉṟa col, maṉattukkuc cāṭciyāka irukkum kaṭavuḷaiyē kuṟikkum.”

1320. “nām muṟṟākap poṟuppēṟka iyalāta nilaiyil, cattiyam ceyvatu kūṭātu.”

1321. “aṟu kayiṟu ūcaleṉṟu maṉam alaital kūṭātu.”

1322. “maraṇa vāyililum vaṉkaṇmai uḷḷam māṟātavarkaḷ vaḷarcci yaṭaiyātavarkaḷ.”

1323. “nām maṭṭum nallavarkaḷāka iruntāl pōtātu. nammaic cuṟṟi iruppavarkaḷum nallavarkaḷāka iruntālē payaṉ uṇṭu.”

1324. “maṟṟavarkaḷiṭam mēlāṇmai koṇarvatē cariyāṉa nirvākattiṟku utaviyāka amaiyum.”

1325. “aṭimaip puttiyuṭaiyavarkaḷ cutantarattai aṉupavikkamāṭṭārkaḷ.”

1326. “piḻaippu naṭatta virumpukiṟavarkaḷ poṟuppuṭaṉ naṭantukoḷḷa māṭṭārkaḷ.”

1327. “kālattil kaṇkāṇittāl iḻappukkaḷait tavirkkalām: īṭṭaṅkaḷaik kūṭṭalām.”

1328. “cintippavaṉ, cintikkātavaṉ collaik kēṭpatu paittiyakkārattaṉam.”

1329. “ciṟu ellaikaḷaittāṉ camāḷikka iyalum.”

1330. “kālam, poṉ-iraṇṭaiyumē aḷantu payaṉpaṭutti miccam piṭikkak kaṟṟuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum.”

1331. “māṉiṭa uṟavukaḷaip parāmarippatu payaṉkaḷait tarum.”

1332. “pukaḻai virumpukiṟavarkaḷiṭam, kañcat taṉam illāmal pukaḻntu pēcuka.”

1333. “valimaiyuṭaiyār tēṭātu pōṉālum ceṉṟu uṟavu koḷvar.”

1334. “uḻaikkātu uṇpavarkku iyaṟkai vaḻaṅkum taṇṭaṉai tāṉ nōy.”

1335. “cila tīrmāṉaṅkaḷ eṭuttālum, ceyaṟpaṭuttappaṭuvatu nallatu.”

1336. “piḻaipaṭac ceyvār varunti māṟṟik koḷḷāmaikkuk kāraṇam, oṉṟu aṟiyāmaiyāka irukkavēṇṭum allatu akantaiyāka irukka vēṇṭum.”

1337. “ciṟiya ciṟiya tavaṟukaḷiliruntē periya tavaṟukaḷ tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa.”

1338. “paṭippikkum āciriyarkaḷ cuṟucuṟuppāka iruntāl, nāṭē cuṟucuṟuppāka iyaṅkum.”

1339. “kaṭamaikaḷaiviṭa kāppi, tēnīr aruntal mutaṉmaip peṟṟuviṭṭāl yārtāṉ kāppāṟṟa iyalum.”

1340. “vayiṟu illātu pōṉāl palar vēlai ceyyamāṭṭārkaḷ.”

1341. “aṉpu illāta iṭattil makiḻcci illai!”

1342. “karuṇaikku viñciya ciṟappuṭaiyatu inta vaiyakattil illai.”

1343. “ovvoru ceṅkallāka aṭukkik kaṭṭiyatil uruvāṉatē māḷikai. atupōla ovvoru ceyalākattāṉ pukaḻpūtta vāḻvu uruppeṟukiṟatu.”

1344. “oruvar vāḻvu, piṟitoruvar vāḻviṉ nalaṉuṭaṉ piṇaintuviṭiṉ vāḻvittu vāḻum paṇpu kāl koḷḷum.”

1345. “oḻuṅkukaḷ paḻakkappaṭum varaiyiltāṉ tollai. paḻakkattiṟku vantu viṭiṉ atu pātukāppāṉa cātaṉam.”

1346. “oruvarukku oruvar utaviyāka iruppatu eṉpatu piṟappiṉ vaḻi amainta kaṭamai.”

1347. “paṭittavarkaḷ ceyta tavaṟukaḷiṉ payaṉē āṭcimuṟaikaḷ.”

1348. “ciṟunīrakam cīrāka iyaṅkātu pōṉāl viḷaiyum tollaiyaip pōṉṟē ciṟiyavarkaḷ cīrāka iyaṅkāviṭiṉum tuṉpam ēṟpaṭum.”

1349. “malaccikkalilum koṭiyatu maṉaccikkal. viraivil tīrvu kāṇpatu nallatu.”

1350. “tām ceyyātu viṭum kāriyaṅkaḷāl ulakam periya tuṉpattiṟku āḷāvatai aṟika.”

1351. “veṟṟu araciyal, ārppāṭṭattil veḷiccam pōṭukiṟatu.”

1352. “naṉmaiyaik kūṭa, naṉmaikkākavē ceyyavēṇṭum. nipantaṉaikaḷukku kaṭṭuppaṭṭu ceyyak kūṭātu.”

1353. “poruṭ celvam pōṟṟum paṇpaṟṟavarkaḷ poruḷuṭaiyarātal aritu.”

1354. “mikavum tarattil kuṟainta maṉitarkaḷ maṟṟavarkaḷaiyum tam aḷavukōlaiyē vaittu aḷappar.”

1355. “camūkac cēvaiyil evvaḷavu makiḻvu irukkiṟatō avvaḷavu nerukkaṭiyum irukkum.”

1356. “nāḷukkoru toḻiṟkūṭam kaṇṭāl nalivu nīṅkum.”

1357. “nirvākiyaiviṭa uḻaippāḷarē uyarntavar.”

1358. “intiya camūka amaippil nirvākikaḷai viṭa, toḻilāḷikaḷ kuṟaivāṉa ūtiyamē peṟukiṟārkaḷ.”

1359. “cutantaram eṉpatu aṉupavikkat takkatu. āṉāl cutantarattai aṉupavikkum tiṟaṉ eḷitil varuvatillai.”

1360. “atikārattāl oṉṟaic ceyvataiviṭa, aṉpāṉa aṇukumuṟaiyāl ceyvatu payaṉuṭaiya paṇpāṭṭu muṟai.”

1361. “kūṭṭuṟavu eṉpatu, irukkum piriviṉaikaḷaik kaṭantu oṉṟupaṭuvatu.”

1362. “iṉṟu kaiyūṭṭu tēciyamayamāki viṭṭatu.”

1363. “viruppam-viḻaivu ivaṟṟil piṟantu vaḷarnta kaiyūṭṭu iṉṟu kaṭṭāyamāki viṭṭatu.”

1364. “maṉitarkaḷ eṭutta tīrmāṉaṅkaḷ ceyaluruk koṇṭiruntāl ulakam corkkamāki irukkum.”

1365. “ceyaṟpāṭṭukku varāta, aracu āṇaikaḷē mikuti.”

1366. “toṭarpuṭaiyār karuttaṟiyāmal oru kōppiṉai muṭippatu tavaṟu.”

1367. “maṉitakulam oṉṟē! ituvē iyaṟtai! piriviṉaikaḷ iyaṟkaikku māṟāṉavai.”

1368. “maṉitakulam muḻumaiyaṭaiya mutaṟpakai vaṟumai."

1369. “vaṟumai ēṟṟattāḻvu - ivaikaḷai vāyilkaḷākak koṇṭē pakai tōṉṟukiṟatu.”

1370. “varalāṟu toṭarntu iyaṅkukiṟatu. inta varalāṟṟiyakkattaik kūrntaṟintu kaṟpavarkaḷ, vāḻkkaiyil veṟṟipeṟuvārkaḷ.”

1371. “intiya cintaṉaiyāḷarkaḷ tuṟavu maṉap pāṉmaiyuṭaiyōrāy, aracuk kaṭṭilukku varāmaiyiṉālēyē namatu nāṭṭuc cintaṉaiyāḷarkaḷ tōṟṟārkaḷ.”

1372. “puttar kūṭa tuṉpa nīkkattiṟku vaḻi tuṟaveṉṟē kaṇṭār.”

1373. “oru poruḷiṉ matippaik kūṭṭuvatu uḻaippu.”

1374. “taṅkam vilai matippuṭaiyatutāṉ. āṉāl, attaṅkattiṉ matippaṟiya, cātā matippuṭaiya māṟṟukkal tāṉ payaṉpaṭukiṟatu. atupōla, uyarnta cintaṉaikaḷ kūṭa carācari maṉitariṭattil tōṟṟuvikkum tākkaṅkaḷaip poṟuttē matippiṭap peṟum.”

1375. “vaḻivaḻi varum paḻakkaṅkaḷukku oru aracāṅkattaiviṭa atika celvākku uṇṭu.”

1376. “intiya cutantarap piracavamē palavīṉamāṉa kaṣṭa nilaitāṉ!”

1377. “āṅkilēyarkaḷ pōyviṭṭālum, avarkaḷ vitaitta iṉa, moḻi, camayap piriviṉai naccu vitaikaḷ ceḻittu vaḷarkiṉṟaṉa.”

1378. “tīvira camayavātikaḷukku inta ulakattai viṭa aṭutta ulakattaip paṟṟiya kavalaiyē atikam.”

1379. “paḻakkam eṉpatu cēṟu. iccēṟṟil cikkiyavarkaḷ eḷitil karaiyēṟa māṭṭārkaḷ.”

1380. “teriyavēṇṭiyaṉa teriyavum, tuṇiya vēṇṭiyaṉa tuṇiyavum ceyya vēṇṭiyaṉa ceyyavum taṭaiyāka iruppavai etuvum tīmaiyēyām.”

1381. “ceyaliṉmaiyāl yāṉ aḻivataiviṭa tāṭcaṇyattāl aḻivatē mikuti.”

1382. “kūṭṭuc camūka vāḻkkaikkut taṉṉait tayārākkik koḷḷātavarkaḷ kūṭṭattil iruntālum taṉi maṉitarē!”

1383. “ulakam muḻutum camanilaic camutāyam tōṉṟiṉālē pōriṭum ulakam tiruntum.”

1384. “vāḻvōriṉum cettār uṇṭu; cettōriṉum vāḻvōr uṇṭu.”

1385. “akkaṟai eṉpatu iṭam nōkkic ceyaṟpaṭum paṇpaṉṟu; iyalpāṉatu.”

1386. “kācukaḷiṉ arumai aṟiyātār, eppōtum celvantar ākār.”

1387. “mēṭaikaḷ amaittalē ippōtu oru piḻaippākiviṭṭatu.”

1388. “irupālum otta, oṉṟaik kūṟiṉālum ēṟka muṉ varātu pōṉāl avarkaḷ niyāyattai maṟukkiṟārkaḷ.”

1389. “cilarukku oṉṟai eppaṭiyum aṭaintu viṭuvatu eṉṟa maṉattiṉ piṉṉaṇiyil karavum vaṉkaṇmaiyum irukkum. ivarkaḷ uṟavu ākātu.”

1390. “uḻaikkāmal cāppiṭum nilappirapukkaḷ, paṭitta aṟiñarkaḷ, pēccukkup paṇam kēṭpataik kuṟai colkiṟārkaḷ.”

1391. “oṉṟait toṭarcciyākak kaṇ kāṇikkātu pōṉāl, putiya cikkalkaḷ tōṉṟum.”

1392. “uḻāta nilam keṭuvatu pōla, paḻakāta uṟavum keṭum.”

1393. “tiṟamaiyaṟṟavarkaḷ, nirvākat talaimaikku ācaippaṭuvatu payaṉaṟṟatu.”

1394. “oṉṟum ceyyātavarkaḷaiviṭa, ceyvatu pōl pāvaṉai ceykiṟavarkaḷ mōcamāṉavarkaḷ.”

1395. “kuḻukkaḷai uruvākka, cērkka muyaṟcippavarkaḷ ētō oru koḷkaikku āyattam ceykiṟārkaḷ eṉpatē karuttu.”

1396. “nālupēr uṭkārntu pēcum iṭattil kūṭṭuc cintaṉai nikaḻum pōtu taṉittum pirintum eṇṇutal caittāṉiṉ ceyal.”

1397. “taṟkālikamāṉa uḻaippukkaḷaik kāṭṭic camāḷippavarkaḷ ciṟu tūṟalkaḷaip pōlat taraiyai naṉaikkalām. avarkaḷāl vaḷattaiyum veṟṟiyaiyum taraviyalātu.”

1398. “oru paṇiyil īṭupaṭṭuḷḷa palar, oruvarai oruvar eṇṇattālum ceyalālum uṟavālum neruṅki varātu pōṉāl antap paṇi ciṟappāka naṭavātu.”

1399. “ēkātipattiya aracukaḷiṉ ceyaṟpāṭu nākarikamāka illai. kolai ceyyum aḷavukku vantuviṭṭaṉar. iṉi inta ulakattai, kaṭavuḷtāṉ kāppāṟṟa vēṇṭum.”

1400. “yārai nampuvata” eṉṟa viṉā, maṉita nākarikam keṭṭu viṭṭatu eṉpataṉ aṭaiyāḷam.”





1401. “vēliyē payirai mēykiṟatu; pātu kāvalaṉē kolkiṟāṉ! kaliyukattiṉ yatārtta nilai!”

1402. “oru talaimai uruvāvataṟku evvaḷavu kālam celavu ākiṟatu? oru noṭiyil kolai ceytu viṭukiṟārkaḷ pāvikaḷ.”

1403. “aṉṉai intirā, intiya nāṭṭiṉ pātukāppukkum vaḷarccikkum ceyta paṇikaḷ niṉaintu niṉaintu pōṟṟattakkavai.”

1404. “viḷakkāta pāttiram tūymaiyāy irātu. āyvu illāta paṇiyil vaḷarcci irātu.”

1405. “kirāmac cūḻalil kuḻantaikaḷ naṉku vaḷarkkappaṭṭāl mētaikaḷ vaḷarvārkaḷ; tōṉṟuvārkaḷ.”

1406. “aṉṉai intirāviṭam tuṇivum uṟutiyum iruntatu! atēpōḻtu nekiḻntu koṭukkum maṉappōkkum iruntatu.”

1407. “mutalāḷittuvam” eṉṟa nañcu uḷḷa varaiyil nallavarkaḷ vāḻamuṭiyātu.”

1408. “mutalāḷi vēṟu; celvantar vēṟu.”

1409. “celvam illāta ēḻaikaḷil kūṭa mutalāḷikaḷ uṇṭu. itu oru kuṇam.”

1410. “pala, ciṟu kaṭappāṭukaḷ muṟaiyāka nikaḻntālē periya kaṭappāṭukaḷ tāmē nikaḻum.”

1411. “enta oru ciṟu nikaḻvaiyum kūrntu kavaṉittu vāḻkkaiyai iyakkuvatu aṟivuṭaimai.”

1412. “cila maṉitarkaḷ tavaṟu ceyvataṟkuk kāraṇaṅkaḷ kūṟa iyalātu. atu avarkaḷuṭaiya cupāvam.”

1413. “uṭuppatu uṇpatu ivaṟṟai eḷimaip paṭuttuvatu aḻukkāṟṟait tavirkka utavi ceyyum.”

1414. “oruvarai aṭiyoṟṟip piṉpaṟṟāmal pōṟṟuvatu oruvitamāṉa uṇarcci. ataṉāl payaṉ illai.”

1415. “intiya araciyalil jaṉanāyakap paṇpu muḻumaittuvam aṭaiyavillai.”

1416. “kacappuṇarcciyum kāḻppuṇarcciyum illātavarkaḷē jaṉanāyakattil kāl koṇṭu niṟka iyalum.”

1417. “intiya araciyalil naparkaḷē mukkiyam. koḷkai, kōṭpāṭukaḷ alla.”

1418. “enta oṉṟum muṟaiyāka naṭavātu pōṉāl iḻappukkaḷē varum.”

1419. “pātukāppu vēlaikaḷ, vēlaiyāka maṭṭum amaiyāmal uṇarvu pūrvamāṉa kaṭamaiyāka amaintāl nallatu.”

1420. “jaṉanāyap paṇpukku iṉa, moḻi, mata vēṟupāṭukaḷ icaintatalla.”

1421. “karuttu vēṟṟumaikaḷ kacappuṇarcciyāka urukkoḷḷutal jaṉanāyaka marapukku icai villātatu.”

ta-10

1422. “oru ellai varaiyiltāṉ poṟuttāṟṟum paṇpu tīmai ellaik kaṭakkum poḻutu kayamaittaṉamākiviṭukiṟatu! kolvatait tavira vēṟu vaḻiyillai!”

1423. “paṇṭum iṉṟum eṉṟum paranta maṉappāṉmai ulakiyalil aṭaintatu tōlviyē!”

1424. “nallavarkaḷaiviṭa tīyavarkaḷ nallavarkaḷāka naṭippatu, kavarcciyāka irukkiṟatu. makkaḷ mayaṅkukiṉṟaṉar.!

1425. “viṭuppil irukkum viruppam, uḻaippilum iruntāl ulakam uruppaṭṭuviṭum.”

1426. “upatēciyātal eḷitu. āṉāl ceyvatu kaṭiṉam.”

1427. “maṉam oru vintaiyāṉa poruḷ. muṟaiyākap paḻakkiṉāl nāy; illaiyāṉāl puli:”

1428. “nōy varātu vāḻtalē oru kalai. ācaikaḷ illāmaṟpōṉāl nōy illai.”

1429. “nūlkaḷaip paṭippatu camaitta cōṟṟaic cāppiṭuvataip pōṉṟatu.”

1430. “iṉṟu ācai, araciyal ellām veṟum uḷḷīṭillāta uṇarccikaḷēyām.”

1431. “talaivariṉ koḷkaikku moṭṭai pōṭukiṟavarkaḷ, talaivar iṟantālum talai moṭṭai pōṭukiṟārkaḷ.”

1432. “palar ēṟṟuk koḷḷum oru ceyal makkaḷāṭci muṟai taḻuviyatē.”

1433. “vāyppukkaḷaip payaṉpaṭutti vaḷarpavarkaḷ cilarē.”

1434. “kacappuṇarcciyai vaḷarttal eḷitu; akaṟṟutal aritu.”

1435. “intiya camūka vāḻkkaiyil vērūṉṟiya piriviṉai eṇṇaṅkaḷai eḷitil akaṟṟa muṭiya villaiyē!”

1436. “niyāyamāṉa oṉṟai aṭaiya eḷiya muyaṟcikaḷē pōtum!”

1437. “piṟar paṟṟi uṉṉiṭam kuṟai colpavarkaḷ niccayam uṉṉaip paṟṟiyum kūṟuvārkaḷ. viḻippāka iru.”

1438. “naṟpaḻakkaṅkaḷ mūlam tāṉ tīyapaḻakkaṅkaḷai akaṟṟa muṭiyum.”

1439. “vāḻkkai, kaḷippukkāka ēṟpaṭṭataṉṟu kaḷippu, vāḻkkaiyil oru pakuti.”

1440. “maṟṟavar kavalaiyai aṟintu māṟṟa muyalupavarkaḷiṉ kavalai tāṉē maṟaiyum.”

1441. “cama vāyppukaḷ iyaṟkaiyāka amaiyattakka vakaiyil camutāya naṭaimuṟai amaintālē nāṭu vaḷarum.”

1442. “pāvikaḷaiyumkūṭa nēcittu kaṭṭup pāṭṭiṟkuḷ vaittuk koḷḷal puṇṇiyam ceytaṟku utaviyāka irukkum.”

1443. “ovvoruvrum avarkaḷāl muṭinta ellai varaiyil taṉi naṭaippōkku. iyalāta ellaiyil camutāyattai nāṭuvar.”

1444. “aṟpa maṉitarkaḷtāṉ” periya maṉitarkaḷākik koṇṭu varukiṉṟaṉar.”

1445. “ciṟupāṉmaiyiṉartāṉ, perumpāṉmaiyiṉarai aṭakkiyāḷkiṉṟaṉar. inta muraṇ pāṭṭukkuk kāraṇam aṟivu peṟutalukku aṉaivarukkum cama vāyppu amaiyāmaiyē yām.”

1446. “eppaṭiyāvatu kāriyattaic ceytu muṭittal eṉṟa vāḻkkai muṟai, naṉṟaṉṟu. ippaṭittāṉ eṉṟu tiṭṭamiṭṭu neṟimuṟaippaṭi amaital vēṇṭum.”

1447. “tīmai ceyyātavarkaḷ aṉaivarum nallavarkaḷ allar; naṉmai ceypavarkaḷē nallavarkaḷ.”

1448. “uyirukku iṉṉal viḷaivikkum paṇiyai maṟṟavarkaḷiṭam ceyyak koṭuppatē maṉita iyaṟkai.”

1449. “vēlai vāṅkum urimaiyaip pōlavē vāḻvaḷikkum kaṭamaiyai mēṟkoḷḷavēṇṭum.”

1450. “kālam, āṟṟal, paṇi mutaliyavaṟṟai muṟaippaṭuttic ceytāl, paṇikaḷaiyum niṟaiya ceyyalām; mikutiyum payaṉuṭaiyatākavum ceyyalām; tappāmalum ceyyalām.”

1451. “ippaṭittāṉ vāḻvēṉ eṉṟu uṇarvu pūrvamāka muṭiveṭuttuviṭṭāl namatu mūḷaiyum uṭalum iyalpākavē ottuḻaikkum.”

1452. “palavīṉamāṉavarkaḷāl camutāyam cīr kēṭaṭaivataiviṭa, palamāṉavarkaḷuṭaiya palam, palavīṉattai īṭuceyyak kūṭiyatāka camutāya uṇarvuṭaṉ vaḷaravillai. paḻutāṉa iyantirattai, paḻutākāta iyantiram iḻuttup pōkavillaiyē!

1453. “cilar nallavarkaḷē! tiṟamai iṉmaiyāl keṭṭavarkaḷ pōlat terivar.”

1454. “viraivu uṇarvu vēṟu; avacarappaṭutal vēṟu. viraivu uṇarvu kāriya cātaṉaiyait tarum. avacarappaṭutal kāriyak kēṭukaḷaiyē tarum.”

1455. “cuṟucuṟuppu vēṟu, paraparappu vēṟu. cuṟu cuṟuppu nallatu. paraparappu nallatalla.”

1456. “iṉṟaiya kuṭumpaṅkaḷil pala, uṭal vayappaṭṭavai; uṇarvuvayappaṭṭavai alla.”

1457. “camamaṟṟa nilaiyil aṉpu tōḻamai, caṉa nāyakam ciṟappatillai.”

1458. “otta nōkkillātār naṭpiṉarāy iruttal aritu.”

1459. “kaḻuvāy muṟaiyāka amaintāl nōy illai; pakai illai!”

1460. “vallāṅku vāḻpavar toṭarntu maṟṟavar vāḻkkaikkut tīṅku ceyvatait taṭukka iyalāta aracu, aracalla.”

1461. “anītiyai etirkkāmal otuṅki vāḻpavarkaḷum aniyāyakkārarkaḷē!”

1462. “aracāṅkap paṇimaṉaikaḷilum eḷiya uḻaippāḷikaḷukku uttaravātam illai.”

1463. “cōviyattil lañcam vāṅkiṉāṉ! itu eppoḻutō nikaḻvatu. āṉāl nam nāṭṭilō lañcam tēciyamayappaṭuttiyāyiṟṟu.”

1464. “palarai moṭṭaiyaṭittu vāḻpavrkūṭa tiruppatikku moṭṭai pōṭukiṉṟaṉar! pāpak kaḻuvāy pōlum.”

1465. “iṉṟaiya araciyalil - vāḻat teriyātavarkaḷē peritum īṭupaṭukiṉṟaṉar.”

1466. “tēvaikku vāṅkiyavarkaḷ, tiruppit tarum poḻutu caṅkaṭappaṭuvatu aṟamaṉṟu.”

1467. “nāḷai eṇṇi ūtiyam koṭukkum muṟai uḻaippaik keṭukkum.”

1468. “uḻaippu, poruḷīṭṭal ākiya iraṇṭilum maṉa niṟaivu koḷḷutal kūṭātu koṇṭāl tēkkanilai varum.”

1469. “āṟṟal iyaṟkai; paṭaippup poruḷaṉṟu. āṟṟalaip payaṉpaṭuttukiṟa muṟaiyilēyē maṉitaṉ veṟṟi peṟukiṟāṉ.”

1470. “uḷḷavar peṟuvār eṉpatu āṉṟōr niyati. iṉṟu ataiyum aḻukkāṟu keṭukkiṟatu.”

1471. “taṉṉaiviṭa maṟṟavar kuṟaivākavē peṟutal vēṇṭum eṉṟa eṇṇam iṉṟu palaraip piṭittāṭṭukiṟatu.”

1472. “iṉṟu eṅkum campantā campantamillāmal pēcip peruntuṉpattai viḷaivikkiṉṟaṉar.”

1473. “iṉṟaiya intiyāvai, vēntalaikkum kolkuṟumpu varuttukiṟatu.”

1474. “aṟivu, āḷviṉaiyil viruppamillātār viḻumiya ciṟappukkaḷait tēṭi alaikiṉṟaṉar.”

1475. “cāvu, - iyaṟkai: āṉāl vāḻāmal vāḻvataiviṭac nāmāka cāvatu mēl.”

1476. “inta ulakam vāḻkkaiyiṉ mēl uḷḷa ācaiyāl entat tuṉpattaiyum eḷitil maṟantuviṭum.”

1477. “vilaṅkukaḷ, taṉakku uṇavuk kiṭaittu viṭṭāl amaiti koṇṭuviṭum; maṟṟa vilaṅkukaḷaip paṟṟik kavalaippaṭātu inta nilaikku iṉṟu maṉitarkaḷum vantuviṭṭārkaḷ.”

1478. “camanilai illāta camutāyattil caṇ nāyaka tattuvam veṟṟi peṟātu.”

1479. “ceypaṇikaḷil pōrāṭṭa uṇarvu illaiyeṉil vāḻkkai eḷitil veṟṟiyait taḻuvātu.”

1480. “intiya camūka vāḻkkaiyil mutumai, pōṟṟappaṭṭatuṇṭu. iṉṟu illai.”

1481. “paṇṭita javahālāl nēru avarkaḷiṉ kuṭumpattiṉ pariṇāma vaḷarcci aṟiviyal cārpuṭaiyatu; cutantarat taṉmaiyuṭaiyatu.”

1482. “iṉṟu kālistāṉ cikkalukkuk kāraṇam pañcāpai, pañcāp, hāriyāṉā eṉṟu pirittatēyām.”

1483. “taṟkālika veṟṟikaḷukkāka tīya caktikaḷuṭaṉ oppantam ceytu koḷḷutal tavaṟu. piṉ viḷaivukaḷ tīmaiyaiyē tarum.”

1484. “kālil muḷ taittuviṭṭatu eṉṟu kēṭkum nilaikku iṉṟaiya camūkam vantu viṭṭatu.”

1485. “kātal vāḻkkaiyil peṇṇukkup pēruḻaippu: peruñcumai! uḻaippu vāḻkkaiyilum appaṭiyē! itu niyāyamaṟṟa taṉmai!”

1486. “kaṭaivītiyil nalla tūymaiyāṉa” cattuḷḷa uṇavu niyāya vilaikkuk kiṭaittāl namatu peṇkaḷ vāḻkkaiyai māṟṟalām; vaḷarkkalām.”

1487. “payam-kōḻaittaṉattukkut tāy! narakattiṉ vāyil!”

1488. “tavaṟukaḷukku añcuvatu vēṟu. taṇṭaṉaikaḷukku añcuvatu vēṟu. iṉṟu taṇṭaṉaikaḷukkē añcukiṉṟaṉar. tavaṟukaḷukku yārum añcuvatillai.”

1489. “oruvartam uṭaṟkaruvikaḷai pūraṇamākap payaṉpaṭuttiṉāl iṉpamāka vāḻalām. itu cattiyam!”

1490. “vāṅkupavar vilai pēcātupōṉāl, viṟpavar viruppamē vilaiyākiviṭum.”

1491. “oru nōkkuṭaiya aracāṅkattil paṇi ceyvōriṭaiyil oruṅkiṇaippiṉmaiyē. nōkkamillāta vāḻkkaiyaik kāṭṭukiṟatu.”

1492. “tiyākam, nākarikam mutaliyaṉa paṟṟik kavalaippaṭātu-ātāyam paṟṟiyē kavalaippaṭutal vipaccārattukku nērāṉatu.”

1493. “camamaṟṟa nilaikaḷ uḷḷavaraiyil naṟpaṇpukaḷ vaḷartal aritu.”

1494. “vāyppukkaḷ tāṉāka varuvaṉa alla; uruvākkik koḷḷappaṭupavai.”

1495. “taṉ tavaṟṟai uṇarum tiṟaṉaṟṟavarkaḷ maṟṟavarkaḷ tavaṟukaḷaip peritupaṭuttupavar.”

1496. “atikārattaic cuvaittal tīmaiyil tīmai.”

1497. “perumpāṉmaiyōr” muṭivellām jaṉanāyakat taṉmai uṭaiyaṉavākātu!”

1498. “vivakārattil uḷappūrvamāṉa camātāṉam kaṇṭāl oḻiya-vivakārattiṟku muṟṟup puḷḷi vaikka muṭiyātu.”

1499. “keṭṭikkāraṉ kiṭaittataic curuṭṭukiṟāṉ; ēmāḷi vāyp paṟai aṟaintu koṇṭirukkiṟāṉ.”

1500. “intiya camutāyattil putiyaṉa cintittavarkaḷ uṇṭu. āṉāl intiya camutāyam putiyaṉa vaṟṟai ēṟkavillai.”





1501. “oruvar ceyyum tavaṟai avaruṭaiya camutāyattiṉ mēl ēṟṟik kūṟutal ākātu. nallatalla.”

1502. “intiyar vāḻnilaiyil māṟṟam ēṟpaṭṭālē-intiyā vaḷarum.”

1503. “takutiyillātaṉvaṟṟiṟku ācaippaṭṭu allalpaṭutal aṟiyāmai.”

1504. “maṟṟavar tuṉpam aṟiyātār mōcamāṉa maṉitarkaḷ.”

1505. “intirā paṭukolaiyil tiṭṭamiṭṭavaṉ veṟṟi peṟṟataṟkuk kāraṇam tiṭṭamiṭṭavaṉiṉ putticālittaṉamalla. maṟṟavarkaḷiṉ kaṭamaiyil kāṭṭiya alaṭciyamē kāraṇamākum.”

1506. “namatu aracāṅkattiṟku cuṟucuṟuppu illāmal iruppatum orukuṟai.”

1507. “kāraṇa kāriyaṅkaḷai ārāyāmal pēcuvatu paittiyakkārattaṉm.”

1508. “oṉṟaic ceytu muṭippatil uḷḷa tollaikaḷai-maṟṟavarkaḷ uṇarvatillai.”

1509. “avacara uṇarcci aṟivaik koṉṟu viṭukiṟatu.”

1510. “pukai vaṇṭip peṭṭiyiṉ mītu ēṟi iruntukoṇṭa kuruvi, pukaivaṇṭi pōkumpōtu tāṉ yāttirai ceytatākak kūṟa iyalātu. kuruvikku ētu aṉupavam?

1511. “uyiriṉ uṇarvukaḷai, vāḻkkaiyiṉ ilakkukaḷai nōkki eṭuttuc cellum karuviyē ilakkiyam.”

1512. “uṇavukku uppup pōla ilakkiya aṉupavattiṟku racaṉai.”

1513. “nuṇmait taṉmaiyuṭaiya uṇarvu nilaiyil amainta aṉupavaṅkaḷ ippōtu illai.”

1514. “pakupporuḷiliruntu nuṇporuḷukkum ceytikaḷiliruntu cintaṉaikkum cellum payiṟci ippōtu illai.”

1515. “namatu camaya niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷil paṇi ceypavarkaḷ, cēkkiḻār paṭaittuk kāṭṭum kuṭumpaṅkaḷaippōla vāḻntālē pōtum. mikutiyum payaṉ viḷaiyum.”

1516. “iruppatil amaitipeṟum-camāḷikkum iyalpu iyalāmaiyiṉ viḷaivēyām. camāḷittalai viṭa-cātittalē ciṟantatu.”

1517. “cakipput taṉmai eṉpatu oruvakaik kōḻaittaṉamēyām.”

1518. “nukartal vāḻkkaiyiṉ kaṭappāṭukaḷil oṉṟu; uriya vilai koṭukkāmal nukartal oḻukkak kēṭu.”

1519. “maṟṟa camayaṅkaḷukku vāyttiruppatu pōla, ayal mūlataṉaṅkaḷ namakku illai. namatu mūlataṉamumkūṭa curaṇṭappaṭukiṟatē tavira-vaḷarkkap peṟavillai.”

1520. “uḻaikkāmal- camūka uḻaippu illāmal vāḻpavarkaḷumkūṭa oruvakaiyil curaṇṭal ceypavarkaḷēyāvar.”

1521. “pūraṇattuvamāṉa uḻaippu tōṉṟāta varaiyil vaiyakattai vaṟumaiyiliruntu mīṭka muṭiyātu.”

1522. “kavaṉamāka - toṭarcciyākac ceyyap peṟum paṇikaḷē payaṉtarum.”

1523. “cutantaram-palar vāḻkkaiyil oḷiyaip parappavillai.”

1524. “maṉitakulam vāḻat terintu koṇṭāl paruva kālamum ēval ceyyum.”

1525. “kuḻantaiyiṉ arumaiyaiyum, poṟuppaiyum uṇarpavarkaḷ-kuṭumpattiṉ eṇṇikkaiyai kaṭṭuppaṭuttik koḷvar.”

1526. “iṉpam eṉpatu kalaviyoṉṟiltāṉ eṉṟu vāḻum vāḻkkai miruka vāḻkkai.”

1527. “oru peruṅkolaikkup piṟaku kūṭa oru iḷaiñaṉait talaivarāka orumaṉatāka tērnteṭukkiṟārkaḷ-iṅku maruttuvamaṉaiyiltāṉ talaivar uḷḷār-ataṟkuḷ ciṇṭupiṭiccaṇṭai!”

1528. “orumaippāṭṭil vaṭapulattār vaḷarntiruppataip pōl tamiḻarkaḷ vaḷaravillai.”

1529. “intiyāvil matattiṉ peyarāl nikaḻum nikaḻccikaḷ-mata nampikkaiyaip poyttup pōkac ceykiṉṟaṉa.”

1530. “urimaikaḷ peṟuvataṟku piriviṉai vēṇṭum eṉpatu poruḷaṟṟatu.”

1531. “vāḻa ācaippaṭukiṉṟaṉar; āṉāl muyaṟcitāṉ illai.”

1532. “naṉṟāka vāḻtalukkut tāy aṟivaṟinta āḷviṉaivē.”

1533. “oruvar mītu paṟṟu eṉpatu avarkaḷaip poṟuttatu illai. avarkaḷuṭaiya koḷkaikaḷaip poṟuttatāka amaital payaṉtarum.”

1534. “iṉṟaiya araciyalil kol kuṟuppuk kārarkaḷē mēvi varukiṉṟaṉar.”

1535. “ceyaliṉmai nōkki veṭkappaṭṭu varuntātār-orupoḻutum ceyal tiṟaṉukku uriyavar ākār.”

1536. “nēṟṟaiya tavaṟukaḷē aḻintuviṭātu. iṉṟu antat tavaṟukaḷai niyāyappaṭuttum muyaṟciyilēyē aḻivu toṭaṅkukiṟatu.”

1537. “tiṟampaṭac ceyyātār-etaṟku? vaiyakattiṟkup poṟaiyāka!”

1538. “parinturaic ceyyum urimai-kāṟṟu pōlap payaṉpaṭuvataṟkalla; marunteṉap payaṉ paṭutalukkēyām,”

1539. “celvattai celavaḻittalaiviṭa - celvākkai celavaḻippatil kavaṉamum-cikkaṉamum tēvai.”

1540. “maṉampōṉa pōkkil nirvāka iyantirattai iyakkiṉāl vipattukkaḷ tavirkka iyalātaṉa vākiviṭum.”

1541. “cilar cila kāriyattai kāriyamākak karutic ceyvatillai. viḷamparattukkākavē ceykiṉṟaṉar.”

1542. “āṟṟoḻukkup pōla-paḻakkaṅkaḷai naṭaimuṟaip paṭuttiṉāl makkaḷ teḷivaṭaivar.”

1543. “lāpanōkkum maṉita ulakattai miruka mākki viṭṭatu.”

1544. “ceyya vēṇṭiya vēlaikaḷ kaṇṇukku etirē illai tēṭip piṭittuē ceyyavēṇṭum.”

1545. “uṭal-uyiraip puttiramāka vaittiruppatupōla kaṭamaikaḷaip pattiramāka-kavaṉamāka ceypavarkaḷ nalla paṇiyāḷarkaḷ.”

1546. “paḻakkamākiviṭa vēṇṭiyavai kūṭa putiyaṉavāka iruppatiṉ kāraṇam īṭupāṭiṉmaiyē”

1547. “iṉṟaiya maṉitaṉai tōlurittup pārttāl oru acal kāṭṭu mirukamākavē kāṭci aḷippāṉ.”

1548. “talaimai” eṉpatu iṉṟaiya varalāṟṟil takutiyaṟṟatu eṉṟa poruḷ payakkum collāka urumāṟiyuḷḷa nilai orntu uṇarattakkatu”

1549. “vaḻikāṭṭutalum-vaḻinaṭattutalum tāṉ talaimaikkuriyap paṇpukaḷ.”

1550. “ciṟanta āḷumai vāyilākat talaimaikku varutal iyalum”.

1551. “nammuṭaiya āṟṟal-nammaic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa poruḷkaḷiṉ āṟṟal-ākiya aṉaittum payaṉpaṭumāṟu peṟṟa nilaiyē āḷumai nilai”

1552. “varukaip pativēṭṭiṉ mūlamē varuvāy varum vāḻkkaiyaip palar virumpukiṉṟaṉar.”

1553. “uṭaliṉ nalattiṟkē uṭai; āṉāl iṉṟaiya nilai... atuvalla.”

1554. “ovvoru kirāmattilum celvam kuvintu kiṭakkiṟatu. uḻaippāl veḷik koṇarvārait tāṉ kāṇōm.”

1555. “eṅkum vēlai niṟaiya irukkiṟatu. āṉāl ceyvōraik kāṇōm; āṉālum vēlai tēṭi alaipavarkaḷum irukkiṟārkaḷ.”

1556. “iyaṟkai-āṇ-peṇ uṇarvukaḷil vēṟupāṭṭaip paṭaikkātapōtu; ēṉ āṇukku oru nīti-peṇṇukku oru nīti?”

1557. “āṇ maṟumaṇam ceyyalām-peṇ maṟumaṇam ceyyakkūṭātu eṉpatu-iyaṟkai niyatikaḷukku muraṇāṉatu.”

1558. “nitiyai nirvakippatil kaṇṭippum, kaṇṇōṭṭamum vēṇṭaṟpālaṉa.”

1559. “aṉaittum iruntum, oṉṟum illāta nilai-nirvākat tāḻcciyaiyē paṟaicāṟṟukiṟatu.”

1560. “vīṭu eppaṭi irukkiṟatu eṉpatil alla. vāḻkkai-vīṭṭil uḷḷavarkaḷ eppaṭi vāḻkiṟārkaḷ eṉpataip poṟuttatēyām.”

1561. “nam talaimuṟaiyil-puṇṇiya pārata pūmiyil matattiṉ peyarāl iraṇṭu paṭukolaikaḷ nikaḻntuviṭṭaṉa. itu veṭkappaṭavēṇṭiya ceyti.”

1562. “aruḷum teyvamē, aṭikkavum ceykiṟatu. itu tavirkka iyalātatu.”

1563. “tiṭṭamiṭṭuc ceytāl; celvam cērntuviṭum.”
